Perfect! Now I have all the information. Let me create a comprehensive report.

## Bug Investigation Report: Publish Flow DB Error

### 1. Publish Flow Files

**Pages:**
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/app/[locale]/publish/page.tsx` — Dashboard listing user's extensions
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/app/[locale]/publish/new/page.tsx` — Entry point for multi-step publish wizard

**Components:**
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx` — Main wizard controller, handles all 3 steps (lines 1-217)
- `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/manifest-form.tsx` — Step 1 form component (lines 1-253)

---

### 2. Server Actions & API Routes

**Server Action File:**
`/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts` (172 lines)

**Step 1 Handler:** `createDraftExtension()` (lines 23-82)

This is the server action called when Step 1 form submits. It:
- Validates input via Zod schema
- Creates two DB records in a transaction:
  1. Inserts into `extensions` table
  2. Inserts into `extensionVersions` table  
  3. Inserts into `extensionTags` table (if tags provided)

```typescript
export async function createDraftExtension(
  raw: ManifestFormValues,
): Promise<CreateDraftResult> {
  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };

  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return { ok: false, error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid input" };
  }
  const data = parsed.data;

  const extensionId = crypto.randomUUID();
  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();

  try {
    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.insert(extensions).values({
        id: extensionId,
        slug: data.slug,
        name: data.name,
        nameZh: data.nameZh ?? null,
        tagline: data.tagline ?? null,
        description: data.description ?? null,
        descriptionZh: data.descriptionZh ?? null,
        category: data.category,
        scope: data.scope,
        funcCat: data.funcCat,
        subCat: data.subCat,
        l2: data.l2 ?? null,
        deptId: data.deptId ?? null,
        homepageUrl: data.homepageUrl || null,
        repoUrl: data.repoUrl || null,
        licenseSpdx: data.licenseSpdx ?? null,
        publisherUserId: session.user.id,
        ownerOrgId: DEFAULT_ORG_ID,
        visibility: "draft",
      });

      await tx.insert(extensionVersions).values({
        id: versionId,
        extensionId,
        version: data.version,
        status: "pending",
      });

      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(
          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),
        );
      }
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown";
    if (msg.includes("unique")) return { ok: false, error: "slug_taken" };
    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };
  }

  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId };
}
```

---

### 3. Where "db_error" is Produced

All 3 occurrences in source code:

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts`

| Line | Context | Function |
|------|---------|----------|
| **78** | Generic DB error in Step 1 (createDraftExtension) | Catch-all for any transaction error except unique constraint violations |
| **113** | Generic DB error in Step 2 (attachFile) | Catch-all when recording file metadata to DB after upload |
| **137** | Generic DB error in Step 3 (submitForReview) | Catch-all when submitting version for review |

**Lines 75-79 (Step 1 - the reported issue):**
```typescript
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown";
    if (msg.includes("unique")) return { ok: false, error: "slug_taken" };
    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };  // ← Line 78
  }
```

---

### 4. How Errors are Surfaced to UI

**Error Display Logic:**
File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx`

**State Management:**
- Line 30: Error stored in React state: `const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);`

**Step 1 Error Handler (lines 34-40):**
```typescript
async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
  setError(null);
  const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
  if (!result.ok) {
    setError(result.error === "slug_taken" ? "Slug already taken — choose another." : result.error);
    return;
  }
  setDraft({ extensionId: result.extensionId, versionId: result.versionId, slug: values.slug, version: values.version });
  setStep(2);
}
```

**Error Rendering (lines 121-125):**
```typescript
{error && (
  <div className="mb-4 rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
    {error}
  </div>
)}
```

**The Bug:** When `createDraftExtension()` throws any non-unique constraint error, the error code returned is literally the string `"db_error"`. Since Step 1 doesn't have a specific translation for this error code, it displays the raw string `"db_error"` to the user instead of a human-readable message.

---

export const extensions = pgTable(
  "extensions",
  {
    id: text().primaryKey(),

    category: extensionCategoryEnum().notNull(),

    scope: extensionScopeEnum().notNull(),
    funcCat: funcCatEnum().notNull(),

    publisherUserId: text().references(() => users.id, {
      onDelete: "set null",
    }),
    ownerOrgId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => organizations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    deptId: text().references(() => departments.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),

    iconColor: text(),
    visibility: visibilityEnum().notNull().default("draft"),

**extensionVersions table (lines 131-150):**

export const extensionVersions = pgTable(
  "extension_versions",
  {
    id: text().primaryKey(),
    extensionId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    version: text().notNull(), // semver
    changelog: text(),
    changelogZh: text(),
    manifestJson: jsonb(),
    bundleFileId: text(),
    status: versionStatusEnum().notNull().default("pending"),
    publishedAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }),
    createdAt: timestamp({ withTimezone: true }).notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [unique("ext_version_unique").on(t.extensionId, t.version)],
);
```

**extensionTags table (lines 172-186):**

export const extensionTags = pgTable(
  "extension_tags",
  {
    extensionId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => extensions.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    tagId: text()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tags.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.extensionId, t.tagId] }),
    index("idx_extension_tags_tag_ext").on(t.tagId, t.extensionId),
  ],
);

---

### 7. Multi-Step Publish Flow Shape

**Step 1: Extension Details (Manifest)**
- File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/manifest-form.tsx`
- **Writes to DB:** YES
- Creates initial draft records:
  - INSERT into `extensions` (visibility='draft')
  - INSERT into `extensionVersions` (status='pending')
  - INSERT into `extensionTags` (if tags provided)
- Server action: `createDraftExtension()`
- Status after: `draft` visibility, `pending` version status

**Step 2: Upload Bundle (File Upload)**
- File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx` (lines 131-175)
- **Writes to DB:** YES, but only metadata
- Uploads ZIP to R2 object storage
- Records file metadata in DB:
  - INSERT into `files` table with r2Key and checksumSha256
  - UPDATE `extensionVersions.bundleFileId` to point to the file
- Server action: `attachFile()`

**Step 3: Review & Submit**
- File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx` (lines 177-213)
- **Writes to DB:** YES
- Transitions version status to `scanning`
- Triggers Inngest job for bundle scanning
- Server action: `submitForReview()` which calls `lib/extensions/state.ts::submit()`

**State Machine:**
```
pending --submit--> scanning --recordScanResult--> ready | rejected --publishVersion--> published
```

---

### Root Cause Analysis

When Step 1 calls `createDraftExtension()` and a database error occurs (other than unique constraint violation on slug), the server action returns `{ ok: false, error: "db_error" }`.

The UI handler at line 38 of `upload-wizard.tsx`:
```typescript
setError(result.error === "slug_taken" ? "Slug already taken — choose another." : result.error);
```

Has a special case for `"slug_taken"` but no i18n mapping for `"db_error"`. It directly assigns the raw error code to the error state, which renders verbatim to the user.

**Possible DB failure causes in Step 1:**
1. Foreign key constraint violation on `deptId` (if invalid department ID provided)
2. Foreign key constraint violation on `ownerOrgId` (if "default" org doesn't exist)
3. Insufficient permissions or database connection issue
4. Transaction timeout or deadlock
5. CHECK constraint violations (if any are defined)
6. Out of disk space

The error message "db_error" gives no indication of which constraint failed or how to fix it.

122	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
123	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
124	
125	  // Load bundleFileId needed by the scan job
126	  const [version] = await db
127	    .select({ bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId })
128	    .from(extensionVersions)
129	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
130	    .limit(1);
131	
132	  if (!version?.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: "no_bundle" };
133	
134	  try {
135	    await submit(versionId);
136	  } catch {
137	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };
138	  }
139	
140	  const { inngest } = await import("@/lib/jobs/client");
141	  await inngest.send({
142	    name: "extension/scan.requested",
143	    data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
144	  });
145	
146	  return { ok: true };
147	}

52	    setUploadProgress("uploading");
53	
54	    // 1. Get presigned URL
55	    const signRes = await fetch("/api/upload/sign", {
56	      method: "POST",
57	      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
58	      body: JSON.stringify({ slug: draft.slug, version: draft.version, contentType: "application/zip", size: file.size }),
59	    });
60	    if (!signRes.ok) { setError("Failed to get upload URL."); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
61	    const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = await signRes.json() as { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string };
62	
63	    // 2. Upload directly to R2
64	    const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" }, body: file });
65	    if (!putRes.ok) { setError("Upload failed. Please try again."); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
66	
67	    // 3. Record file in DB (checksum placeholder — real checksum done by Inngest scan)
68	    const attachResult = await attachFile(draft.versionId, r2Key, file.size, "pending");
69	    if (!attachResult.ok) { setError(attachResult.error); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
70	
71	    setUploadProgress("done");
72	    setFileUploaded(true);
73	  }
74	
75	  async function handleSubmit() {
76	    if (!draft) return;
77	    setSubmitting(true);
78	    setError(null);
79	    const result = await submitForReview(draft.versionId);
80	    setSubmitting(false);
81	    if (!result.ok) { setError(result.error); return; }
82	    setSubmitted(true);
83	  }
84	
85	  const stepLabels = [tw("step1Title"), tw("step2Title"), tw("step3Title")];
86	
87	  if (submitted) {
88	    return (
89	      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-16 text-center">
90	        <CheckCircle className="h-12 w-12 text-green-500" />
91	        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">{tw("successTitle")}</h2>
92	        <p className="text-muted-foreground max-w-sm">{tw("successBody")}</p>
93	        <Link href="/publish" className="mt-4 text-sm underline text-primary">
94	          {tw("backToDashboard")}
95	        </Link>
96	      </div>
97	    );
98	  }
99	
100	  return (
101	    <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
102	      {/* Step indicator */}
103	      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-8">
104	        {([1, 2, 3] as Step[]).map((s) => (
105	          <div key={s} className="flex items-center gap-2">
106	            <span className={`flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-semibold border ${
107	              s === step
108	                ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary"
109	                : s < step
110	                  ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700 border-green-300"
111	                  : "border-border text-muted-foreground"
112	            }`}>{s < step ? "✓" : s}</span>
113	            <span className={`text-sm ${s === step ? "font-medium" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
114	              {stepLabels[s - 1]}
115	            </span>
116	            {s < 3 && <span className="text-muted-foreground mx-1">›</span>}
117	          </div>
118	        ))}
119	      </div>
120	
121	      {error && (
122	        <div className="mb-4 rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
123	          {error}
124	        </div>
125	      )}
126	
127	      {/* Step 1: Manifest */}
128	      {step === 1 && <ManifestForm onSubmit={handleManifestSubmit} />}
129	
130	      {/* Step 2: File upload */}
131	      {step === 2 && (
132	        <div className="space-y-6">
133	          <div
134	            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-border p-12 text-center cursor-pointer hover:border-primary transition-colors"
135	            onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
136	          >
137	            {uploadProgress === "done" ? (
138	              <>
139	                <CheckCircle className="h-10 w-10 text-green-500 mb-3" />
140	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("uploaded")}</p>
141	              </>
142	            ) : uploadProgress === "uploading" ? (
143	              <>
144	                <Upload className="h-10 w-10 text-primary mb-3 animate-pulse" />
145	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("uploading")}</p>
146	              </>
147	            ) : (

149	                <Upload className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground mb-3" />
150	                <p className="font-medium">{tu("dropzone")}</p>
151	                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{tu("maxSize")}</p>
152	              </>
153	            )}
154	            <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept=".zip" className="hidden" onChange={handleFileChange} />
155	          </div>
156	
157	          <div className="flex justify-between">
158	            <button
159	              type="button"
160	              onClick={() => setStep(1)}

162	            >

164	            </button>

170	            >
171	              {tw("nextButton")}
172	            </button>
173	          </div>
174	        </div>
175	      )}
176	
177	      {/* Step 3: Review & submit */}
178	      {step === 3 && draft && (

180	          <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-6 space-y-2 text-sm">
181	            <div className="flex justify-between">

192	            </div>

194

1	import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
2	
3	import { COLLECTIONS } from "@/lib/data/collections";
4	import { DEPARTMENTS } from "@/lib/data/departments";
5	import { EXTENSIONS } from "@/lib/data/extensions";
6	import { db } from "@/lib/db/client";
7	import {
8	  departments,
9	  extensions,
10	  extensionTags,
11	  organizations,
12	  tags,
13	} from "@/lib/db/schema";
14	import { TAG_LABELS } from "@/lib/tags";
15	import type { Department } from "@/types";
16	
17	// Single-tenant default for v1; multi-org UI lives behind this for later.
18	const ORG_ID = "default";
19	
20	interface FlatDept {
21	  id: string;
22	  orgId: string;
23	  parentId: string | null;
24	  name: string;
25	  nameZh: string;
26	  pathDepth: number;
27	}
28	
29	function flattenDepts(
30	  list: Department[],
31	  depth = 0,
32	  parentId: string | null = null,
33	  out: FlatDept[] = [],
34	): FlatDept[] {
35	  for (const d of list) {
36	    out.push({
37	      id: d.id,
38	      orgId: ORG_ID,
39	      parentId,
40	      name: d.name,
41	      nameZh: d.nameZh,
42	      pathDepth: depth,
43	    });
44	    if (d.children) flattenDepts(d.children, depth + 1, d.id, out);
45	  }
46	  return out;
47	}
48	
49	function slugify(name: string): string {
50	  return name
51	    .toLowerCase()
52	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
53	    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
54	}
55	
56	const CATEGORY_LABELS = {
57	  skills: "skill",
58	  mcp: "MCP server",
59	  slash: "slash command",
60	  plugins: "plugin",
61	} as const;
62	
63	/**
64	 * Placeholder README content. Real READMEs will arrive via the upload wizard
65	 * (Phase 10) once the manifest spec is wired up.
66	 */
67	function generateReadme(ext: (typeof EXTENSIONS)[number]): string {
68	  const kind = CATEGORY_LABELS[ext.category];
69	  const installCmd =
70	    ext.category === "slash"
71	      ? `agentcenter install ${slugify(ext.name)}`
72	      : `agentcenter install ${slugify(ext.name)}`;
73	  return `# ${ext.name}
74	
75	${ext.desc}
76	
77	## Overview
78	
79	${ext.name} is a ${kind} published by **${ext.author}**. It plugs into your
80	agent runtime so you can ${ext.desc.toLowerCase().replace(/\.$/, "")} without
81	hand-rolling integrations.
82	
83	## Install
84	
85	\`\`\`bash
86	${installCmd}
87	\`\`\`
88	
89	After installing, restart your agent to pick up the new ${kind}.
90	
91	## Compatibility
92	
93	- **Claude** — supported on the latest agent runtimes
94	- **Other agents** — install paths can be customized via \`agentcenter config\`
95	
96	## Tags
97	
98	${ext.tags.map((t) => `\`${t}\``).join(" · ")}
99	`;
100	}
101	
102	async function main() {
103	  console.log("seed: starting");
104	
105	  // Idempotent wipe of catalog data. CASCADE follows FKs through the whole
106	  // org→dept→extension→junction graph. Leaves users/sessions/tags untouched
107	  // for orgs; we then separately wipe tags.

3	// Slug shape, exported so client-side form validators don't redefine it.
4	export const SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;
5	export const SEMVER_PATTERN = /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/;
6	
7	// Single source of truth for what makes an extension manifest valid.
8	// Mirrors `docs/manifest-spec.md`. Form and bundle schemas below derive
9	// from this core; nothing should redefine these constraints elsewhere.
10	export const ExtensionManifestCore = z.object({
11	  slug: z
12	    .string()
13	    .min(3)
14	    .max(64)
15	    .regex(SLUG_PATTERN, "Lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens only"),
16	  name: z.string().min(2).max(80),
17	  nameZh: z.string().max(80).optional(),
18	  version: z.string().regex(SEMVER_PATTERN, "Must be semver (e.g. 1.0.0)"),
19	  category: z.enum(["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"]),
20	  scope: z.enum(["personal", "org", "enterprise"]),
21	  funcCat: z.enum(["workTask", "business", "tools"]),
22	  subCat: z.string().min(1).max(60),
23	  l2: z.string().max(60).optional(),
24	  description: z.string().min(1).max(280),
25	  descriptionZh: z.string().max(280).optional(),
26	  tagline: z.string().max(120).optional(),
27	});
28	
29	// Form: every core field plus the UI-only extras the publish wizard collects.
30	export const ManifestFormSchema = ExtensionManifestCore.extend({
31	  tagIds: z.array(z.string()).max(8),
32	  deptId: z.string().optional(),
33	  homepageUrl: z.string().url().optional().or(z.literal("")),
34	  repoUrl: z.string().url().optional().or(z.literal("")),
35	  licenseSpdx: z.string().max(40).optional(),
36	});

38	export type ManifestFormValues = z.infer<typeof ManifestFormSchema>;

40	// Bundle: same fields, lifted into the [extension] / [categorization] TOML

45	    name: true,
46	    nameZh: true,
47	    version: true,
48	    category: true,
49	    scope: true,

> AGENT

Now I understand the picture. Let me write up what I found before making changes.

## Root cause analysis

Two real problems compound to produce the unhelpful "db_error":

**Bug 1 — empty-string `deptId` causes FK violation** (`lib/actions/publish.ts:53`):

```ts
deptId: data.deptId ?? null,
```

`??` only nulls out `null`/`undefined`. The form's `deptId` defaults to `""` (manifest-form.tsx:46) and stays `""` if the user picks "— none —". Empty string then violates the FK to `departments.id`. The same line uses `||` for `homepageUrl`/`repoUrl` — `deptId` was missed.

**Bug 2 — opaque error reporting** (`lib/actions/publish.ts:75-79`):

```ts
} catch (err) {
  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "unknown";
  if (msg.includes("unique")) return { ok: false, error: "slug_taken" };
  return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };
}
```

Everything that isn't a unique-constraint violation collapses into `"db_error"`, with no server log. The UI then renders that raw string (upload-wizard.tsx:38) because there's no i18n mapping. Step 2 and Step 3 have the same shape.

## What I propose

1. Fix the `deptId` empty-string bug (one-character change: `??` → `||`).
2. Server-side: `console.error` the real error (with PG code if available) so future failures are diagnosable.
3. Map common Postgres error codes (`23503` foreign-key, `23505` unique, `23502` not-null) to specific error codes: `slug_taken`, `invalid_dept`, `invalid_tag`, `org_missing`, `db_error` (true fallback).
4. Add `publish.errors.*` keys in `en.json` + `zh.json` and have the wizard render `t(\`publish.errors.${code}\`)` with a sensible fallback.
5. Apply the same treatment to `attachFile` and `submitForReview` so Step 2/3 aren't opaque either.

Sound good? I'll proceed unless you want to redirect.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts",
  "content": "\"use server\";\n\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { headers } from \"next/headers\";\n\nimport { auth } from \"@/lib/auth\";\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\nimport {\n  extensions,\n  extensionVersions,\n  extensionTags,\n  files,\n} from \"@/lib/db/schema\";\nimport { submit } from \"@/lib/extensions/state\";\nimport { ManifestFormSchema, type ManifestFormValues } from \"@/lib/validators/manifest\";\n\nconst DEFAULT_ORG_ID = \"default\";\n\n// Postgres error codes we care about. The `code` field is set by node-postgres\n// on DatabaseError instances; we narrow with `unknown` rather than importing\n// pg types so this stays runtime-only.\nfunction pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (typeof err === \"object\" && err !== null && \"code\" in err) {\n    const code = (err as { code: unknown }).code;\n    if (typeof code === \"string\") return code;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (typeof err === \"object\" && err !== null && \"constraint\" in err) {\n    const c = (err as { constraint: unknown }).constraint;\n    if (typeof c === \"string\") return c;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.\n// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type\n// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.\nfunction classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  if (code === \"23505\") return \"slug_taken\"; // unique_violation\n  if (code === \"23503\") {\n    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"dept\")) return \"invalid_dept\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"tag\")) return \"invalid_tag\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"org\")) return \"org_missing\";\n    return \"invalid_reference\";\n  }\n  if (code === \"23502\") return \"missing_required\"; // not_null_violation\n  return \"db_error\";\n}\n\nexport type CreateDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }\n  | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nexport async function createDraftExtension(\n  raw: ManifestFormValues,\n): Promise<CreateDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);\n  if (!parsed.success) {\n    return { ok: false, error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? \"invalid_input\" };\n  }\n  const data = parsed.data;\n\n  const extensionId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();\n\n  try {\n    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n      await tx.insert(extensions).values({\n        id: extensionId,\n        slug: data.slug,\n        name: data.name,\n        nameZh: data.nameZh || null,\n        tagline: data.tagline || null,\n        description: data.description || null,\n        descriptionZh: data.descriptionZh || null,\n        category: data.category,\n        scope: data.scope,\n        funcCat: data.funcCat,\n        subCat: data.subCat,\n        l2: data.l2 || null,\n        // Empty strings are FK violations against departments.id — coerce to null.\n        deptId: data.deptId || null,\n        homepageUrl: data.homepageUrl || null,\n        repoUrl: data.repoUrl || null,\n        licenseSpdx: data.licenseSpdx || null,\n        publisherUserId: session.user.id,\n        ownerOrgId: DEFAULT_ORG_ID,\n        visibility: \"draft\",\n      });\n\n      await tx.insert(extensionVersions).values({\n        id: versionId,\n        extensionId,\n        version: data.version,\n        status: \"pending\",\n      });\n\n      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {\n        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(\n          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),\n        );\n      }\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    const code = classifyDraftError(err);\n    console.error(\"[publish] createDraftExtension failed\", {\n      code,\n      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),\n      constraint: pgConstraint(err),\n      slug: data.slug,\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n    return { ok: false, error: code };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId };\n}\n\nexport type AttachFileResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nexport async function attachFile(\n  versionId: string,\n  r2Key: string,\n  size: number,\n  checksumSha256: string,\n): Promise<AttachFileResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();\n\n  try {\n    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n      await tx.insert(files).values({\n        id: fileId,\n        r2Key,\n        size: BigInt(size),\n        checksumSha256,\n        mimeType: \"application/zip\",\n        scanStatus: \"pending\",\n      });\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ bundleFileId: fileId })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[publish] attachFile failed\", {\n      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),\n      constraint: pgConstraint(err),\n      versionId,\n      r2Key,\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n    return { ok: false, error: \"db_error\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport type SubmitResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nexport async function submitForReview(versionId: string): Promise<SubmitResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  // Load bundleFileId needed by the scan job\n  const [version] = await db\n    .select({ bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!version?.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: \"no_bundle\" };\n\n  try {\n    await submit(versionId);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[publish] submitForReview failed\", {\n      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),\n      versionId,\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n    return { ok: false, error: \"db_error\" };\n  }\n\n  const { inngest } = await import(\"@/lib/jobs/client\");\n  await inngest.send({\n    name: \"extension/scan.requested\",\n    data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },\n  });\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(extensions.createdAt);\n}\n\nexport async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  return row?.status ?? null;\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Summary of changes:

**`lib/actions/publish.ts`**
- Bug fix: `deptId: data.deptId ?? null` → `data.deptId || null` so the empty string from the "— none —" option becomes `NULL` instead of FK-violating against `departments.id`. This is almost certainly what was firing for you. Same `||` treatment applied to other optional text fields for consistency.
- Inspect `err.code` (Postgres error code) and `err.constraint` to classify failures into specific codes: `slug_taken` (23505), `invalid_dept` / `invalid_tag` / `org_missing` / `invalid_reference` (23503), `missing_required` (23502), `db_error` as the true fallback.
- `console.error` now logs the real error with PG code, constraint name, and message — so the next failure is diagnosable from the server log instead of a black box.
- Same logging/classification applied to `attachFile` and `submitForReview`.

**`components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx`**
- Added a `describeError(code)` helper that maps known codes through `t("publish.errors.<code>")`, with a `fallback` message for anything unknown — so a raw `"db_error"` string can never again land in the UI.
- Inline upload error strings ("Failed to get upload URL.", "Upload failed. Please try again.") moved to i18n.

**`lib/i18n/messages/{en,zh}.json`**
- New `publish.errors` namespace covering all the codes above plus `unauthenticated`, `no_bundle`, `uploadSign`, `uploadFailed`, and `fallback`. Both EN and ZH translations.

`bun run typecheck` and `bun run lint` both pass. Most likely root cause for your specific failure: empty-string `deptId`. If after the fix you still hit an error, the server log will now show exactly which constraint failed (e.g. `org_missing` if the `"default"` organization row is absent — i.e. seed wasn't run).

The user-facing message is now friendly, but the underlying DB call is still failing — my classifier didn't match the actual error code, so it fell through to `db_error`. The new server-side logging captures the real PG code and constraint.

Two things I need from you to nail this:

**1. Check your dev server terminal** (wherever you're running `bun dev` / `next dev`) for a line starting with `[publish] createDraftExtension failed`. Paste me that log line — it will include `pgCode`, `constraint`, and `message`. That tells us exactly what's failing.

**2. While we wait** — a couple of common causes worth checking on your end:

- Did you run the seed script? `bun run scripts/seed.ts` (or whatever your project's seed command is). Without it, the `organizations` row with id=`"default"` is missing, so `ownerOrgId: "default"` will FK-violate. Same goes for the `tags` table — if you selected any tags but the table is empty, that violates the `extension_tags.tagId` FK.
- Are migrations applied? `bunx drizzle-kit migrate` (or `push`). If the tables don't exist at all, you'd get a "relation does not exist" error (PG code `42P01`) which my classifier doesn't currently map.

Want me to also surface the raw PG details to the UI when `NODE_ENV === "development"`? That way we wouldn't need to hop into the logs to debug — but it should stay dev-only since exposing constraint names in prod is mildly risky.

19	// Postgres error codes we care about. The `code` field is set by node-postgres
20	// on DatabaseError instances; we narrow with `unknown` rather than importing
21	// pg types so this stays runtime-only.
22	function pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {
23	  if (typeof err === "object" && err !== null && "code" in err) {
24	    const code = (err as { code: unknown }).code;
25	    if (typeof code === "string") return code;
26	  }
27	  return undefined;
28	}
29	
30	function pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {
31	  if (typeof err === "object" && err !== null && "constraint" in err) {
32	    const c = (err as { constraint: unknown }).constraint;
33	    if (typeof c === "string") return c;
34	  }
35	  return undefined;
36	}
37	
38	// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.
39	// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type
40	// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.
41	function classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {
42	  const code = pgErrorCode(err);
43	  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);
44	  if (code === "23505") return "slug_taken"; // unique_violation
45	  if (code === "23503") {
46	    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed
47	    if (constraint?.includes("dept")) return "invalid_dept";
48	    if (constraint?.includes("tag")) return "invalid_tag";
49	    if (constraint?.includes("org")) return "org_missing";
50	    return "invalid_reference";
51	  }
52	  if (code === "23502") return "missing_required"; // not_null_violation
53	  return "db_error";
54	}
55

130	export async function attachFile(
131	  versionId: string,
132	  r2Key: string,
133	  size: number,
134	  checksumSha256: string,
135	): Promise<AttachFileResult> {
136	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
137	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
138	
139	  const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
140	
141	  try {
142	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
143	      await tx.insert(files).values({
144	        id: fileId,
145	        r2Key,
146	        size: BigInt(size),
147	        checksumSha256,
148	        mimeType: "application/zip",
149	        scanStatus: "pending",
150	      });
151	      await tx
152	        .update(extensionVersions)
153	        .set({ bundleFileId: fileId })
154	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
155	    });
156	  } catch (err) {
157	    console.error("[publish] attachFile failed", {
158	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
159	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
160	      versionId,
161	      r2Key,
162	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
163	    });
164	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };
165	  }
166	
167	  return { ok: true };
168	}
169	
170	export type SubmitResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };
171	
172	export async function submitForReview(versionId: string): Promise<SubmitResult> {
173	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
174	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
175	
176	  // Load bundleFileId needed by the scan job
177	  const [version] = await db
178	    .select({ bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId })
179	    .from(extensionVersions)
180	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
181	    .limit(1);
182	
183	  if (!version?.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: "no_bundle" };
184	
185	  try {
186	    await submit(versionId);
187	  } catch (err) {
188	    console.error("[publish] submitForReview failed", {
189	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
190	      versionId,
191	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
192	    });
193	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error" };
194	  }
195	
196	  const { inngest } = await import("@/lib/jobs/client");
197	  await inngest.send({
198	    name: "extension/scan.requested",
199	    data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
200	  });
201	
202	  return { ok: true };
203	}
204	
205	export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {
206	  return db
207	    .select({
208	      id: extensions.id,
209	      slug: extensions.slug,
210	      name: extensions.name,
211	      category: extensions.category,
212	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
213	      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,
214	    })
215	    .from(extensions)
216	    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))
217	    .orderBy(extensions.createdAt);
218	}

Returns undefined in production so we never leak\n// PG codes / constraint names to end users. Format is compact so it fits\n// under the friendly message in the UI.\nfunction devErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\") return undefined;\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (code) parts.push(`pg=${code}`);\n  if (constraint) parts.push(`constraint=${constraint}`);\n  parts.push(message);\n  return parts.join(\" · \");\n}"
}

51	  // codes fall back to a generic message rather than leaking raw codes (e.g.
52	  // "db_error") to the user.
53	  function describeError(code: string): string {
54	    return KNOWN_ERROR_CODES.has(code) ? te(code) : te("fallback");
55	  }
56	
57	  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
58	    setError(null);
59	    const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
60	    if (!result.ok) {
61	      setError(describeError(result.error));
62	      return;
63	    }
64	    setDraft({ extensionId: result.extensionId, versionId: result.versionId, slug: values.slug, version: values.version });
65	    setStep(2);
66	  }
67	
68	  async function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
69	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
70	    if (!file || !draft) return;
71	
72	    if (!file.name.endsWith(".zip")) { setError(tu("errorType")); return; }
73	    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tu("errorSize")); return; }
74	    setError(null);
75	    setUploadProgress("uploading");
76	
77	    // 1. Get presigned URL
78	    const signRes = await fetch("/api/upload/sign", {
79	      method: "POST",
80	      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
81	      body: JSON.stringify({ slug: draft.slug, version: draft.version, contentType: "application/zip", size: file.size }),
82	    });
83	    if (!signRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadSign")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
84	    const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = await signRes.json() as { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string };
85	
86	    // 2. Upload directly to R2
87	    const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" }, body: file });

89	
90	    // 3. Record file in DB (checksum placeholder — real checksum done by Inngest scan)
91	    const attachResult = await attachFile(draft.versionId, r2Key, file.size, "pending");
92	    if (!attachResult.ok) { setError(describeError(attachResult.error)); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
93	
94	    setUploadProgress("done");
95	    setFileUploaded(true);
96	  }
97	
98	  async function handleSubmit() {
99	    if (!draft) return;
100	    setSubmitting(true);
101	    setError(null);
102	    const result = await submitForReview(draft.versionId);
103	    setSubmitting(false);
104	    if (!result.ok) { setError(describeError(result.error)); return; }
105	    setSubmitted(true);
106	  }

---
# Options and configuration

## `neon(...)` function

The `neon(...)` function returns a tagged-template query function:

```typescript
import { neon } from '@neondatabase/serverless';
const sql = neon(process.env.DATABASE_URL);

const rows = await sql`SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ${postId}`;
// -> [{ id: 12, title: "My post", ... }]
```

These queries are composable. For example, this has the same effect:

```javascript
const whereClause = sql`WHERE id = ${postId}`;
const [post] = await sql`SELECT * FROM posts ${whereClause}`;
```

If you need to pass SQL in a variable, not as a template-string literal, use the `query()` property of the template function and numbered placeholders (`$1`, `$2`, etc.):

```javascript
const q = 'SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = $1';
const [post] = await sql.query(q, [postId]);
```

If you need to interpolate _trusted_ arbitrary strings, such as the names of columns or tables, use the `unsafe()` property of the template function:

```javascript
const table = condition ? 'some_posts' : 'other_posts';
const [post] =
  await sql`SELECT * FROM ${sql.unsafe(table)} WHERE id = ${postId}`;
```

By default, only the rows resulting from the provided SQL query are returned, and they're returned as an array of objects where the keys are column names. However, you can customise the return format of the query function using the configuration options `fullResults` and `arrayMode`. These options are available both on the `neon(...)` function and on the `query()` function that's a property of the function it returns.

### `arrayMode: boolean`

When `arrayMode` is true, rows are returned as an array of arrays instead of an array of objects:

```typescript
import { neon } from '@neondatabase/serverless';
const sql = neon(process.env.DATABASE_URL, { arrayMode: true });
const rows = await sql`SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ${postId}`;
// -> [[12, "My post", ...]]
```

Or, with the same effect:

```typescript
import { neon } from '@neondatabase/serverless';
const sql = neon(process.env.DATABASE_URL);
const rows = await sql.query('SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = $1', [postId], {
  arrayMode: true,
});
// -> [[12, "My post", ...]]
```
```

> AGENT

Now I can see the actual SQL — but the underlying PG error (the actual failure reason) is in `err.cause`, not `err.message`. Drizzle wraps the original error: the `Failed query: ...` text is the wrapper's message, and the real error (with `.code` and `.constraint`) sits at `err.cause`. My `pgErrorCode`/`pgConstraint` helpers don't unwrap, so they returned `undefined` and the classifier fell through to `db_error`.

Two-part fix:

1. Walk the `cause` chain in the helpers so we extract the real PG code/constraint.
2. Show the underlying message in the dev detail too, not just Drizzle's outer wrapper.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01AHT79pUCzPbpSe5k4fZroz
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts",
  "old_string": "// Postgres error codes we care about. The `code` field is set by node-postgres\n// on DatabaseError instances; we narrow with `unknown` rather than importing\n// pg types so this stays runtime-only.\nfunction pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (typeof err === \"object\" && err !== null && \"code\" in err) {\n    const code = (err as { code: unknown }).code;\n    if (typeof code === \"string\") return code;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (typeof err === \"object\" && err !== null && \"constraint\" in err) {\n    const c = (err as { constraint: unknown }).constraint;\n    if (typeof c === \"string\") return c;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n// Dev-only debug string. Returns undefined in production so we never leak\n// PG codes / constraint names to end users. Format is compact so it fits\n// under the friendly message in the UI.\nfunction devErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\") return undefined;\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (code) parts.push(`pg=${code}`);\n  if (constraint) parts.push(`constraint=${constraint}`);\n  parts.push(message);\n  return parts.join(\" · \");\n}",
  "new_string": "// Walk the `cause` chain looking for a property that matches the predicate.\n// Drizzle wraps the underlying node-postgres `DatabaseError` (which carries\n// `code` and `constraint`) in its own error whose message is the SQL that\n// failed. The PG details live on `err.cause` (and sometimes deeper).\nfunction findInCauseChain<T>(\n  err: unknown,\n  pick: (e: object) => T | undefined,\n): T | undefined {\n  let current: unknown = err;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5 && current && typeof current === \"object\"; i++) {\n    const got = pick(current);\n    if (got !== undefined) return got;\n    current = (current as { cause?: unknown }).cause;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n// Postgres error code (e.g. \"23503\" foreign_key_violation). Set on\n// node-postgres `DatabaseError` instances.\nfunction pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && typeof (e as { code: unknown }).code === \"string\") {\n      return (e as { code: string }).code;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\nfunction pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\n      \"constraint\" in e &&\n      typeof (e as { constraint: unknown }).constraint === \"string\"\n    ) {\n      return (e as { constraint: string }).constraint;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// The underlying PG message (e.g. 'insert or update on table \"extensions\"\n// violates foreign key constraint ...'), not Drizzle's wrapper which is\n// just the SQL.\nfunction pgMessage(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && e instanceof Error) return e.message;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// Dev-only debug string. Returns undefined in production so we never leak\n// PG codes / constraint names to end users. Format is compact so it fits\n// under the friendly message in the UI.\nfunction devErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\") return undefined;\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  const message =\n    pgMessage(err) ?? (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (code) parts.push(`pg=${code}`);\n  if (constraint) parts.push(`constraint=${constraint}`);\n  parts.push(message);\n  return parts.join(\" · \");\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Migration 0002 was authored as a hand-applied side-script (note the file header: "Run once against the Neon database"). It's not registered in `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`, so `drizzle-kit migrate` skips it entirely.

To apply it now, run **one** of these against your Neon DB (whichever you find easier):

```bash
# Option A — via psql (uses your DATABASE_URL from .env)
psql "$DATABASE_URL" -f drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql
```

Or paste the contents of `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql` into the Neon SQL Editor and run it.

After that, restart dev and Step 1 should work.

Separately — the fact that this is a hand-applied side-script outside the drizzle journal is a setup landmine: any fresh `bun run db:migrate` won't pick it up. Worth fixing in a follow-up (either add a small `scripts/apply-fts.ts` runner or wire it into the drizzle journal properly), but that's a different concern from the publish bug. Want me to do that as a follow-up after we confirm Step 1 works?

The SQL uses IF NOT EXISTS guards, so it's\n// safe to re-run.\n\nimport { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { resolve } from \"node:path\";\n\nimport { sql } from \"drizzle-orm\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/lib/db/client\";\n\nasync function main() {\n  const file = resolve(process.cwd(), \"drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql\");\n  const text = readFileSync(file, \"utf8\");\n\n  console.log(`apply-fts: executing ${file}`);\n  await db.execute(sql.raw(text));\n  console.log(\"apply-fts: done\");\n}\n\nmain()\n  .then(() => process.exit(0))\n  .catch((err) => {\n    console.error(\"apply-fts: failed\");\n    console.error(err);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n"

import { defineConfig } from "vitest/config";
import { resolve } from "path";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  test: {
    // Default environment for lib/ and cli/ unit tests.
    // components/ tests carry a `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` pragma.
    environment: "node",
    setupFiles: ["./vitest.setup.ts"],
    // Unit tests live next to their source files in lib/ and cli/.
    // components/ client components are tested here too.
    // app/ RSC pages and routes are covered by tests/e2e/ (Playwright).
    include: ["lib/**/*.test.ts", "cli/**/*.test.ts", "components/**/*.test.tsx"],
    env: {
      // Prevents neon() from throwing at import time in unit tests.
      // No real DB connection is made — queries are never executed.
      DATABASE_URL: "REDACTED",
    },
    coverage: {
      provider: "v8",
      include: ["lib/**/*.ts", "cli/**/*.ts"],
      exclude: ["lib/db/schema/**", "lib/jobs/client.ts", "**/*.test.ts"],
    },
  },
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      "@": resolve(__dirname, "."),
    },
  },
});

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish-errors.ts",
  "content": "// Pure helpers for decoding the errors thrown by Drizzle / node-postgres\n// when the publish-wizard server actions write to the database.\n//\n// Drizzle wraps the underlying `DatabaseError` so `err.message` is the\n// SQL that failed and the real `code` / `constraint` / message live on\n// `err.cause`. Anything that classifies these errors must walk that chain.\n\n// Walk the `cause` chain looking for a property that matches the predicate.\n// Capped at 5 levels to short-circuit accidental cycles.\nfunction findInCauseChain<T>(\n  err: unknown,\n  pick: (e: object) => T | undefined,\n): T | undefined {\n  let current: unknown = err;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5 && current && typeof current === \"object\"; i++) {\n    const got = pick(current);\n    if (got !== undefined) return got;\n    current = (current as { cause?: unknown }).cause;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n// Postgres error code, e.g. \"23503\" (foreign_key_violation). Set by\n// node-postgres on `DatabaseError` instances.\nexport function pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && typeof (e as { code: unknown }).code === \"string\") {\n      return (e as { code: string }).code;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\nexport function pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\n      \"constraint\" in e &&\n      typeof (e as { constraint: unknown }).constraint === \"string\"\n    ) {\n      return (e as { constraint: string }).constraint;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// The underlying PG message (e.g. 'insert or update on table \"extensions\"\n// violates foreign key constraint ...'), not Drizzle's wrapper which is\n// just the SQL.\nexport function pgMessage(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && e instanceof Error) return e.message;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.\n// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type\n// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.\nexport function classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  if (code === \"23505\") return \"slug_taken\"; // unique_violation\n  if (code === \"23503\") {\n    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"dept\")) return \"invalid_dept\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"tag\")) return \"invalid_tag\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"org\")) return \"org_missing\";\n    return \"invalid_reference\";\n  }\n  if (code === \"23502\") return \"missing_required\"; // not_null_violation\n  return \"db_error\";\n}\n\n// Dev-only debug string. Returns undefined in production so we never leak\n// PG codes / constraint names to end users. Format is compact so it fits\n// under the friendly message in the UI.\nexport function devErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === \"production\") return undefined;\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  const message =\n    pgMessage(err) ?? (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (code) parts.push(`pg=${code}`);\n  if (constraint) parts.push(`constraint=${constraint}`);\n  parts.push(message);\n  return parts.join(\" · \");\n}\n"
}

<tool_use_error>String to replace not found in file.
String: import { submit } from "@/lib/extensions/state";
import { ManifestFormSchema, type ManifestFormValues } from "@/lib/validators/manifest";

const DEFAULT_ORG_ID = "default";

// Walk the `cause` chain looking for a property that matches the predicate.
// Drizzle wraps the underlying node-postgres `DatabaseError` (which carries
// `code` and `constraint`) in its own error whose message is the SQL that
// failed. The PG details live on `err.cause` (and sometimes deeper).
function findInCauseChain<T>(
  err: unknown,
  pick: (e: object) => T | undefined,
): T | undefined {
  let current: unknown = err;
  for (let i = 0; i < 5 && current && typeof current === "object"; i++) {
    const got = pick(current);
    if (got !== undefined) return got;
    current = (current as { cause?: unknown }).cause;
  }
  return undefined;
}

// Postgres error code (e.g. "23503" foreign_key_violation). Set on
// node-postgres `DatabaseError` instances.
function pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {
  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {
    if ("code" in e && typeof (e as { code: unknown }).code === "string") {
      return (e as { code: string }).code;
    }
    return undefined;
  });
}

function pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {
  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {
    if (
      "constraint" in e &&
      typeof (e as { constraint: unknown }).constraint === "string"
    ) {
      return (e as { constraint: string }).constraint;
    }
    return undefined;
  });
}

// The underlying PG message (e.g. 'insert or update on table "extensions"
// violates foreign key constraint ...'), not Drizzle's wrapper which is
// just the SQL.
function pgMessage(err: unknown): string | undefined {
  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {
    if ("code" in e && e instanceof Error) return e.message;
    return undefined;
  });
}

// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.
// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type
// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.
function classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {
  const code = pgErrorCode(err);
  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);
  if (code === "23505") return "slug_taken"; // unique_violation
  if (code === "23503") {
    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed
    if (constraint?.includes("dept")) return "invalid_dept";
    if (constraint?.includes("tag")) return "invalid_tag";
    if (constraint?.includes("org")) return "org_missing";
    return "invalid_reference";
  }
  if (code === "23502") return "missing_required"; // not_null_violation
  return "db_error";
}

// Dev-only debug string. Returns undefined in production so we never leak
// PG codes / constraint names to end users. Format is compact so it fits
// under the friendly message in the UI.
function devErrorDetail(err: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") return undefined;
  const code = pgErrorCode(err);
  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);
  const message =
    pgMessage(err) ?? (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (code) parts.push(`pg=${code}`);
  if (constraint) parts.push(`constraint=${constraint}`);
  parts.push(message);
  return parts.join(" · ");
}

  "content": "import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\nimport {\n  classifyDraftError,\n  devErrorDetail,\n  pgConstraint,\n  pgErrorCode,\n  pgMessage,\n} from \"./publish-errors\";\n\n// Shape that mirrors a node-postgres DatabaseError: a real Error instance\n// with `code` (5-char SQLSTATE), `constraint`, and a descriptive message.\nfunction pgError(opts: {\n  code: string;\n  constraint?: string;\n  message?: string;\n}): Error & { code: string; constraint?: string } {\n  const err = new Error(opts.message ?? \"db error\") as Error & {\n    code: string;\n    constraint?: string;\n  };\n  err.code = opts.code;\n  if (opts.constraint) err.constraint = opts.constraint;\n  return err;\n}\n\n// Mirrors how Drizzle wraps the underlying DatabaseError: the outer Error's\n// message is the SQL that failed, and the real PG error sits at `cause`.\nfunction drizzleWrapped(cause: Error): Error {\n  return new Error(\n    \"Failed query: insert into \\\"extensions\\\" ...\\nparams: a,b,c\",\n    { cause },\n  );\n}\n\ndescribe(\"pgErrorCode / pgConstraint / pgMessage\", () => {\n  it(\"reads code and constraint from a top-level pg error\", () => {\n    const err = pgError({\n      code: \"23505\",\n      constraint: \"extensions_slug_unique\",\n      message: \"duplicate key\",\n    });\n    expect(pgErrorCode(err)).toBe(\"23505\");\n    expect(pgConstraint(err)).toBe(\"extensions_slug_unique\");\n    expect(pgMessage(err)).toBe(\"duplicate key\");\n  });\n\n  // Regression: this is the bug that made every error look like \"db_error\"\n  // after the neon-http -> neon-serverless switch. Drizzle now wraps the\n  // pg error, so the helpers must walk `cause`.\n  it(\"unwraps a Drizzle-style wrapper to reach the inner pg error\", () => {\n    const inner = pgError({\n      code: \"23503\",\n      constraint: \"extensions_owner_org_id_organizations_id_fk\",\n      message: 'fk violation on \"organizations\"',\n    });\n    const wrapped = drizzleWrapped(inner);\n\n    // The outer error's own message is the SQL — never confuse it with the\n    // pg-level message.\n    expect(wrapped.message).toContain(\"Failed query:\");\n\n    expect(pgErrorCode(wrapped)).toBe(\"23503\");\n    expect(pgConstraint(wrapped)).toBe(\n      \"extensions_owner_org_id_organizations_id_fk\",\n    );\n    expect(pgMessage(wrapped)).toBe('fk violation on \"organizations\"');\n  });\n\n  it(\"walks more than one level of cause\", () => {\n    const inner = pgError({ code: \"23502\", message: \"not null\" });\n    const mid = new Error(\"middle wrapper\", { cause: inner });\n    const outer = new Error(\"outer wrapper\", { cause: mid });\n\n    expect(pgErrorCode(outer)).toBe(\"23502\");\n    expect(pgMessage(outer)).toBe(\"not null\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns undefined when no pg fields are present anywhere\", () => {\n    const err = new Error(\"plain error\");\n    expect(pgErrorCode(err)).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(pgConstraint(err)).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(pgMessage(err)).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"tolerates non-Error values without throwing\", () => {\n    expect(pgErrorCode(\"not an object\")).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(pgErrorCode(null)).toBeUndefined();\n    expect(pgErrorCode(undefined)).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"classifyDraftError\", () => {\n  it(\"maps unique_violation to slug_taken\", () => {\n    expect(\n      classifyDraftError(\n        pgError({ code: \"23505\", constraint: \"extensions_slug_unique\" }),\n      ),\n    ).toBe(\"slug_taken\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"maps fk violations by constraint name\", () => {\n    expect(\n      classifyDraftError(\n        pgError({ code: \"23503\", constraint: \"extensions_dept_id_fk\" }),\n      ),\n    ).toBe(\"invalid_dept\");\n    expect(\n      classifyDraftError(\n        pgError({ code: \"23503\", constraint: \"extension_tags_tag_id_fk\" }),\n      ),\n    ).toBe(\"invalid_tag\");\n    expect(\n      classifyDraftError(\n        pgError({ code: \"23503\", constraint: \"extensions_owner_org_id_fk\" }),\n      ),\n    ).toBe(\"org_missing\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"falls back to invalid_reference for an unrecognized fk constraint\", () => {\n    expect(\n      classifyDraftError(\n        pgError({ code: \"23503\", constraint: \"extensions_some_other_fk\" }),\n      ),\n    ).toBe(\"invalid_reference\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"maps not_null_violation to missing_required\", () => {\n    expect(classifyDraftError(pgError({ code: \"23502\" }))).toBe(\n      \"missing_required\",\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns db_error for unknown codes (e.g. undefined_column)\", () => {\n    expect(classifyDraftError(pgError({ code: \"42703\" }))).toBe(\"db_error\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"classifies through a Drizzle wrapper (regression)\", () => {\n    const inner = pgError({\n      code: \"23503\",\n      constraint: \"extensions_owner_org_id_organizations_id_fk\",\n    });\n    expect(classifyDraftError(drizzleWrapped(inner))).toBe(\"org_missing\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns db_error > AGENT

Pushed. Branch summary:

- `c627099` initial fix (deptId, error classification, i18n)
- `9201bdb` dev-mode error detail in UI
- `68aa677` driver swap to neon-serverless Pool
- `a2116f2` walk Drizzle's `cause` chain
- `1042fad` `db:apply-fts` script
- `80cf3b5` extract helpers + 15-case unit test

The PR (#19) now carries the whole investigation. The test pins the regression we just hit — if anyone later swaps drivers or upgrades Drizzle and the error-wrapping shape changes, the classifier test will catch it before users see opaque `db_error` again.

Things I deliberately didn't test:
- The driver swap and `apply-fts` script — both are infrastructure, not logic.
- `createDraftExtension` itself — would require a real DB to exercise the actual FK paths. The classifier (now tested) is the failure surface that broke; the action is just glue around `db.transaction`.

That can leave `uploadProgress` on `\"uploading\"` or `submitting` stuck `true` with no user-facing error.\n\u003e \n\u003e  \n\u003e \n\u003e \u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003e \u003csummary\u003eProposed fix\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003e \n\u003e ```diff\n\u003e  async function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent\u003cHTMLInputElement\u003e) {\n\u003e    const file = e.target.files?.[0];\n\u003e    if (!file || !draft) return;\n\u003e @@\n\u003e    clearError();\n\u003e    setUploadProgress(\"uploading\");\n\u003e +  try {\n\u003e +    // 1. > AGENT

CodeRabbit posted one review with 2 actionable comments and 5 nitpicks. Triaged below — I've split into "real bugs worth fixing", "small tightenings", and "skip".

**Real bugs (worth fixing):**

1. **Stuck loading state on thrown errors** — `handleManifestSubmit`/`handleFileChange`/`handleSubmit` `await` without `try/catch`. If a server action or `fetch` throws (network blip, unhandled exception), `uploadProgress` stays `"uploading"` or `submitting` stays `true` forever with no user feedback. *Add try/catch + finally.*
2. **Stale upload state when re-doing Step 1** — going Back from Step 2 → editing Step 1 → submitting creates a *new* draft (new versionId) but the old `fileUploaded=true` / `uploadProgress="done"` persist, letting the user advance to Step 3 without uploading the new bundle. *Reset upload state in `handleManifestSubmit` on success.*
3. **23505 always classifies as `slug_taken`** — but the `extension_versions` table also has a unique constraint (`ext_version_unique` on `(extensionId, version)`). A duplicate-version retry would mis-message as "slug already taken". *Check constraint name like the FK branch does.*
4. **Zod errors leak English text past i18n** — when `safeParse` fails, we return `parsed.error.issues[0]?.message` as the error code, which the wizard then runs through `describeError` → `KNOWN_ERROR_CODES` won't match → falls through to generic `fallback` (and Zod's English text never reaches the user). *Return code `"invalid_input"` and put the Zod messages in `detail`.*

**Small tightenings (defensible, low cost):**

5. `pgMessage` — restrict to 5-char SQLSTATE so a wrapper carrying a Node-style code (e.g. `"ECONNRESET"`) doesn't shadow the real PG message.
6. `||` → `=== "" ? null : value` for the optional text fields — none of these would legitimately be `"0"`, but explicit is clearer.
7. Fail-fast guard for missing `DATABASE_URL` in `lib/db/client.ts`.

**Skip:**

8. Reusing the classifier in `attachFile`/`submitForReview` — the friendly message ends up the same and the dev-detail already shows the PG code; low value.
9. `scripts/apply-fts.ts` resolving path relative to script vs cwd — the only invocation path is `bun run db:apply-fts` which already runs from repo root; not worth the boilerplate.

I'd do 1-4 + 5 + 7. (#6 is borderline — happy to do it for consistency, but it's purely cosmetic.) Sound right? I'll proceed unless you want to redirect.

Set by\n// node-postgres on `DatabaseError` instances.\nexport function pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && typeof (e as { code: unknown }).code === \"string\") {\n      return (e as { code: string }).code;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\nexport function pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\n      \"constraint\" in e &&\n      typeof (e as { constraint: unknown }).constraint === \"string\"\n    ) {\n      return (e as { constraint: string }).constraint;\n    }\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// The underlying PG message (e.g. 'insert or update on table \"extensions\"\n// violates foreign key constraint ...'), not Drizzle's wrapper which is\n// just the SQL.\nexport function pgMessage(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (\"code\" in e && e instanceof Error) return e.message;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.\n// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type\n// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.\nexport function classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  if (code === \"23505\") return \"slug_taken\"; // unique_violation\n  if (code === \"23503\") {\n    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"dept\")) return \"invalid_dept\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"tag\")) return \"invalid_tag\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"org\")) return \"org_missing\";\n    return \"invalid_reference\";\n  }\n  if (code === \"23502\") return \"missing_required\"; // not_null_violation\n  return \"db_error\";\n}",
  "new_string": "// Postgres SQLSTATE codes are exactly 5 characters. Restricting to that\n// shape stops us from picking up Node-style string codes (e.g.\n// \"ECONNRESET\") that may appear on outer wrappers in the cause chain.\nconst SQLSTATE_RE = /^[A-Z0-9]{5}$/;\n\n// Postgres error code, e.g. \"23503\" (foreign_key_violation). Set by\n// node-postgres on `DatabaseError` instances.\nexport function pgErrorCode(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    const c = (e as { code?: unknown }).code;\n    if (typeof c === \"string\" && SQLSTATE_RE.test(c)) return c;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\nexport function pgConstraint(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    const c = (e as { constraint?: unknown }).constraint;\n    if (typeof c === \"string\") return c;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// The underlying PG message (e.g. 'insert or update on table \"extensions\"\n// violates foreign key constraint ...'), not Drizzle's wrapper which is\n// just the SQL. Gated on a 5-char SQLSTATE so a wrapper with a Node-style\n// code can't shadow the real PG message deeper in the chain.\nexport function pgMessage(err: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return findInCauseChain(err, (e) => {\n    if (!(e instanceof Error)) return undefined;\n    const c = (e as { code?: unknown }).code;\n    if (typeof c === \"string\" && SQLSTATE_RE.test(c)) return e.message;\n    return undefined;\n  });\n}\n\n// Map a thrown DB error to a stable error code the UI can translate.\n// Codes are strings (not enums) so adding new ones doesn't require a type\n// migration. Keep in sync with `publish.errors.*` in messages/{en,zh}.json.\nexport function classifyDraftError(err: unknown): string {\n  const code = pgErrorCode(err);\n  const constraint = pgConstraint(err);\n  if (code === \"23505\") {\n    // unique_violation: which unique index? `extensions.slug` is `slug`,\n    // `ext_version_unique` is on (extension_id, version).\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"slug\")) return \"slug_taken\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"version\")) return \"version_taken\";\n    return \"unique_conflict\";\n  }\n  if (code === \"23503\") {\n    // foreign_key_violation: figure out which FK failed\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"dept\")) return \"invalid_dept\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"tag\")) return \"invalid_tag\";\n    if (constraint?.includes(\"org\")) return \"org_missing\";\n    return \"invalid_reference\";\n  }\n  if (code === \"23502\") return \"missing_required\"; // not_null_violation\n  return \"db_error\";\n}"
}

26	export type CreateDraftResult =
27	  | { ok: true; extensionId: string; versionId: string }
28	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
29	
30	export async function createDraftExtension(
31	  raw: ManifestFormValues,
32	): Promise<CreateDraftResult> {
33	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
34	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
35	
36	  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);
37	  if (!parsed.success) {
38	    return { ok: false, error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "invalid_input" };
39	  }
40	  const data = parsed.data;
41	
42	  const extensionId = crypto.randomUUID();
43	  const versionId = crypto.randomUUID();
44	
45	  try {
46	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
47	      await tx.insert(extensions).values({
48	        id: extensionId,
49	        slug: data.slug,
50	        name: data.name,
51	        nameZh: data.nameZh || null,
52	        tagline: data.tagline || null,
53	        description: data.description || null,
54	        descriptionZh: data.descriptionZh || null,
55	        category: data.category,
56	        scope: data.scope,
57	        funcCat: data.funcCat,
58	        subCat: data.subCat,
59	        l2: data.l2 || null,
60	        // Empty strings are FK violations against departments.id — coerce to null.
61	        deptId: data.deptId || null,
62	        homepageUrl: data.homepageUrl || null,
63	        repoUrl: data.repoUrl || null,
64	        licenseSpdx: data.licenseSpdx || null,
65	        publisherUserId: session.user.id,
66	        ownerOrgId: DEFAULT_ORG_ID,
67	        visibility: "draft",
68	      });
69	
70	      await tx.insert(extensionVersions).values({
71	        id: versionId,
72	        extensionId,
73	        version: data.version,
74	        status: "pending",
75	      });
76	
77	      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {
78	        await tx.insert(extensionTags).values(
79	          data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })),
80	        );
81	      }
82	    });
83	  } catch (err) {
84	    const code = classifyDraftError(err);
85	    console.error("[publish] createDraftExtension failed", {
86	      code,
87	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
88	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
89	      slug: data.slug,
90	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
91	    });
92	    return { ok: false, error: code, detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
93	  }
94	
95	  return { ok: true, extensionId, versionId };
96	}
97	
98	export type AttachFileResult =
99	  | { ok: true }
100	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
101	
102	export async function attachFile(
103	  versionId: string,
104	  r2Key: string,
105	  size: number,
106	  checksumSha256: string,
107	): Promise<AttachFileResult> {
108	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
109	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
110	
111	  const fileId = crypto.randomUUID();
112	
113	  try {
114	    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
115	      await tx.insert(files).values({
116	        id: fileId,
117	        r2Key,
118	        size: BigInt(size),
119	        checksumSha256,
120	        mimeType: "application/zip",
121	        scanStatus: "pending",
122	      });
123	      await tx
124	        .update(extensionVersions)
125	        .set({ bundleFileId: fileId })
126	        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId));
127	    });
128	  } catch (err) {
129	    console.error("[publish] attachFile failed", {
130	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
131	      constraint: pgConstraint(err),
132	      versionId,
133	      r2Key,
134	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
135	    });
136	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
137	  }
138	
139	  return { ok: true };
140	}
141	
142	export type SubmitResult =
143	  | { ok: true }
144	  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };
145	
146	export async function submitForReview(versionId: string): Promise<SubmitResult> {
147	  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });
148	  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: "unauthenticated" };
149	
150	  // Load bundleFileId needed by the scan job
151	  const [version] = await db
152	    .select({ bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId })
153	    .from(extensionVersions)
154	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
155	    .limit(1);
156	
157	  if (!version?.bundleFileId) return { ok: false, error: "no_bundle" };
158	
159	  try {
160	    await submit(versionId);
161	  } catch (err) {
162	    console.error("[publish] submitForReview failed", {
163	      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),
164	      versionId,
165	      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
166	    });
167	    return { ok: false, error: "db_error", detail: devErrorDetail(err) };
168	  }
169	
170	  const { inngest } = await import("@/lib/jobs/client");
171	  await inngest.send({
172	    name: "extension/scan.requested",
173	    data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },
174	  });
175	
176	  return { ok: true };
177	}
178	
179	export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {
180	  return db
181	    .select({
182	      id: extensions.id,
183	      slug: extensions.slug,
184	      name: extensions.name,
185	      category: extensions.category,
186	      visibility: extensions.visibility,
187	      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,
188	    })
189	    .from(extensions)
190	    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))
191	    .orderBy(extensions.createdAt);
192	}
193	
194	export async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {
195	  const [row] = await db
196	    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })
197	    .from(extensionVersions)
198	    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))
199	    .limit(1);
200	  return row?.status ?? null;
201	}
