36	export function UploadWizard() {
37	  const tw = useTranslations("publish.wizard");
38	  const tu = useTranslations("publish.upload");
39	  const te = useTranslations("publish.errors");
40	
41	  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(1);
42	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<DraftState | null>(null);
43	  const [fileUploaded, setFileUploaded] = useState(false);
44	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
45	  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
46	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
47	  const [errorDetail, setErrorDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);
48	  const [uploadProgress, setUploadProgress] = useState<"idle" | "uploading" | "done">("idle");
49	  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
50	
51	  function clearError() {
52	    setError(null);
53	    setErrorDetail(null);
54	  }
55	
56	  // Translate a server-action error code into a localized message. Unknown
57	  // codes fall back to a generic message rather than leaking raw codes (e.g.
58	  // "db_error") to the user.
59	  function describeError(code: string): string {
60	    return KNOWN_ERROR_CODES.has(code) ? te(code) : te("fallback");
61	  }
62	
63	  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
64	    clearError();
65	    const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
66	    if (!result.ok) {
67	      setError(describeError(result.error));
68	      setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
69	      return;
70	    }
71	    setDraft({ extensionId: result.extensionId, versionId: result.versionId, slug: values.slug, version: values.version });
72	    setStep(2);
73	  }
74	
75	  async function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
76	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
77	    if (!file || !draft) return;
78	
79	    if (!file.name.endsWith(".zip")) { setError(tu("errorType")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
80	    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tu("errorSize")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
81	    clearError();
82	    setUploadProgress("uploading");
83	
84	    // 1. Get presigned URL
85	    const signRes = await fetch("/api/upload/sign", {
86	      method: "POST",
87	      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
88	      body: JSON.stringify({ slug: draft.slug, version: draft.version, contentType: "application/zip", size: file.size }),
89	    });
90	    if (!signRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadSign")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
91	    const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = await signRes.json() as { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string };
92	
93	    // 2. Upload directly to R2
94	    const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" }, body: file });
95	    if (!putRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadFailed")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
96	
97	    // 3. Record file in DB (checksum placeholder — real checksum done by Inngest scan)
98	    const attachResult = await attachFile(draft.versionId, r2Key, file.size, "pending");
99	    if (!attachResult.ok) {
100	      setError(describeError(attachResult.error));
101	      setErrorDetail(attachResult.detail ?? null);
102	      setUploadProgress("idle");
103	      return;
104	    }
105	
106	    setUploadProgress("done");
107	    setFileUploaded(true);
108	  }
109	
110	  async function handleSubmit() {
111	    if (!draft) return;
112	    setSubmitting(true);
113	    clearError();
114	    const result = await submitForReview(draft.versionId);
115	    setSubmitting(false);
116	    if (!result.ok) {
117	      setError(describeError(result.error));
118	      setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
119	      return;
120	    }
121	    setSubmitted(true);
122	  }
123	
124	  const stepLabels = [tw("step1Title"), tw("step2Title"), tw("step3Title")];
125	
126	  if (submitted) {
127	    return (
128	      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-16 text-center">
129	        <CheckCircle className="h-12 w-12 text-green-500" />
130	        <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">{tw("successTitle")}</h2>

Upload directly to R2\n      const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: \"PUT\", headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/zip\" }, body: file });\n      if (!putRes.ok) { setError(te(\"uploadFailed\")); setUploadProgress(\"idle\"); return; }\n\n      // 3. > AGENT

Pushed (06e2739). Summary of what landed this round:

**Real bugs fixed:**
1. Wizard handlers wrapped in `try/catch + finally` — a thrown error can no longer strand `uploadProgress="uploading"` or `submitting=true`.
2. `resetUploadState()` runs after a fresh draft is created, so re-editing Step 1 no longer carries the previous bundle's "uploaded" badge into Step 2.
3. `classifyDraftError` now reads the unique constraint name: `*_slug_*` → `slug_taken`, `ext_version_unique` → `version_taken`, fallback → `unique_conflict`. Added EN+ZH messages for the two new codes.
4. Zod failures return `error: "invalid_input"` + `detail: <joined messages>` instead of leaking Zod's English string as the code; the wizard now localizes the headline and still shows what failed.

**Tightenings:**
5. `pgErrorCode` and `pgMessage` require a 5-char SQLSTATE, so a wrapper with `code: "ECONNRESET"` can't shadow the real PG error. New regression test added.
6. `||` empty-string coercion → explicit `emptyToNull` helper, so a legitimate `"0"` would survive.
7. `lib/db/client.ts` throws at module load if `DATABASE_URL` is missing, rather than blowing up at first query.

237 tests pass (added 1 SQLSTATE-walk test, modified the unique-violation classifier test). Skipped #8 (classifying `attachFile`/`submitForReview` errors — same friendly message either way) and #9 (resolving `apply-fts.ts` path relative to script — only invoked via `bun run` from repo root). Both noted in my prior message.

3	import { authenticateBearerToken } from "@/lib/api/auth";

11	const BodySchema = z.object({
12	  slug: z.string().min(3).max(80),
13	  version: z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/),
14	  contentType: z.string().default("application/zip"),
15	  size: z.number().int().positive().max(MAX_BUNDLE_SIZE),
16	});

18	export async function POST(req: Request) {
19	  // Accept both cookie session (web wizard) and Bearer token (CLI future use)
20	  const bearerUser = await authenticateBearerToken(req);
21	  const cookieSession = bearerUser ? null : await getSession();
22	  if (!bearerUser && !cookieSession) {
23	    return Response.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
24	  }
25	
26	  let body: unknown;
27	  try {
28	    body = await req.json();
29	  } catch {
30	    return Response.json({ error: "Invalid JSON" }, { status: 400 });
31	  }
32	
33	  const parsed = BodySchema.safeParse(body);
34	  if (!parsed.success) {
35	    return Response.json({ error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message }, { status: 422 });
36	  }
37	  const { slug, version, contentType, size: _size } = parsed.data;
38	
39	  const key = bundleKey(slug, version);
40	
41	  let uploadUrl: string;
42	  try {
43	    uploadUrl = await generatePresignedPutUrl(key, contentType);
44	  } catch {
45	    return Response.json({ error: "Could not generate upload URL" }, { status: 503 });
46	  }
47	
48	  return Response.json({ uploadUrl, r2Key: key });
49	}
50

1	import { S3Client, PutObjectCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
2	import { getSignedUrl } from "@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner";
3	
4	const R2_ACCOUNT_ID = process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID ?? "";
5	const R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID = process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID ?? "";
6	const R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY = process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY ?? "";
7	const R2_BUCKET = process.env.R2_BUCKET ?? "agentcenter-bundles";
8	
9	function getClient(): S3Client {
10	  return new S3Client({
11	    region: "auto",
12	    endpoint: `https://${R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
13	    credentials: {
14	      accessKeyId: R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID,
15	      secretAccessKey: R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY,
16	    },
17	  });
18	}
19	
20	export async function generatePresignedPutUrl(
21	  key: string,
22	  contentType: string,
23	  expiresInSeconds = 300,
24	): Promise<string> {
25	  const client = getClient();
26	  const command = new PutObjectCommand({
27	    Bucket: R2_BUCKET,
28	    Key: key,
29	    ContentType: contentType,
30	  });
31	  return getSignedUrl(client, command, { expiresIn: expiresInSeconds });
32	}
33	
34	export async function generatePresignedGetUrl(
35	  key: string,
36	  expiresInSeconds = 3600,
37	): Promise<string> {
38	  const { GetObjectCommand } = await import("@aws-sdk/client-s3");
39	  const client = getClient();
40	  const command = new GetObjectCommand({ Bucket: R2_BUCKET, Key: key });
41	  return getSignedUrl(client, command, { expiresIn: expiresInSeconds });
42	}
43	
44	export function bundleKey(slug: string, version: string): string {
45	  return `bundles/${slug}/${version}/bundle.zip`;
46	}
47

> AGENT

I can see two likely culprits without seeing the console:

1. **R2 CORS preflight rejection** — `fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT" })` would throw `TypeError: Failed to fetch` rather than return `!ok`. The project has a `r2:cors` script, so this has bitten before.
2. **R2 env vars missing/wrong** — `R2_ACCOUNT_ID`/`R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` default to empty strings; presign would succeed (returns a malformed URL) and the actual PUT would fail.

Either way, the right move is to make the client-side caught errors surface their detail in dev — same pattern we used for server actions. Then we don't need to keep diving into devtools.

77	    setFileUploaded(false);

82	  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
83	    clearError();
84	    try {
85	      const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
86	      if (!result.ok) {
87	        setError(describeError(result.error));
88	        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
89	        return;
90	      }
91	      setDraft({
92	        extensionId: result.extensionId,
93	        versionId: result.versionId,
94	        slug: values.slug,
95	        version: values.version,
96	      });
97	      resetUploadState();
98	      setStep(2);
99	    } catch (err) {
100	      console.error("[publish] handleManifestSubmit threw", err);
101	      setError(te("fallback"));
102	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
103	    }
104	  }
105	
106	  async function handleFileChange(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
107	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
108	    if (!file || !draft) return;
109	
110	    if (!file.name.endsWith(".zip")) { setError(tu("errorType")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
111	    if (file.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tu("errorSize")); setErrorDetail(null); return; }
112	    clearError();
113	    setUploadProgress("uploading");
114	
115	    try {
116	      // 1. Get presigned URL
117	      const signRes = await fetch("/api/upload/sign", {
118	        method: "POST",
119	        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
120	        body: JSON.stringify({ slug: draft.slug, version: draft.version, contentType: "application/zip", size: file.size }),
121	      });
122	      if (!signRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadSign")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
123	      const { uploadUrl, r2Key } = await signRes.json() as { uploadUrl: string; r2Key: string };
124	
125	      // 2. Upload directly to R2
126	      const putRes = await fetch(uploadUrl, { method: "PUT", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/zip" }, body: file });
127	      if (!putRes.ok) { setError(te("uploadFailed")); setUploadProgress("idle"); return; }
128	
129	      // 3. Record file in DB (checksum placeholder — real checksum done by Inngest scan)
130	      const attachResult = await attachFile(draft.versionId, r2Key, file.size, "pending");
131	      if (!attachResult.ok) {
132	        setError(describeError(attachResult.error));
133	        setErrorDetail(attachResult.detail ?? null);
134	        setUploadProgress("idle");
135	        return;
136	      }
137	
138	      setUploadProgress("done");
139	      setFileUploaded(true);
140	    } catch (err) {
141	      console.error("[publish] handleFileChange threw", err);
142	      setError(te("fallback"));
143	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
144	      setUploadProgress("idle");
145	    }
146	  }
147	
148	  async function handleSubmit() {
149	    if (!draft) return;
150	    setSubmitting(true);
151	    clearError();
152	    try {
153	      const result = await submitForReview(draft.versionId);
154	      if (!result.ok) {
155	        setError(describeError(result.error));
156	        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
157	        return;
158	      }
159	      setSubmitted(true);

1	/**
2	 * Apply CORS rules to the R2 bundle bucket.
3	 *
4	 * Run once after creating the bucket, and again when the production domain changes:
5	 *
6	 *   PRODUCTION_URL=https://agentcenter.app bun run scripts/set-r2-cors.ts
7	 *
8	 * Requires R2_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID, R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY in the environment
9	 * (or .env.local).
10	 */
11	import { S3Client, PutBucketCorsCommand, GetBucketCorsCommand } from "@aws-sdk/client-s3";
12	
13	const R2_ACCOUNT_ID = process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID;
14	const R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID = process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID;
15	const R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY = process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY;
16	const R2_BUCKET = process.env.R2_BUCKET ?? "agentcenter-bundles";
17	const PRODUCTION_URL = process.env.PRODUCTION_URL;
18	
19	if (!R2_ACCOUNT_ID || !R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID || !R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY) {
20	  console.error(
21	    "Missing required env vars: R2_ACCOUNT_ID, R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID, R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY",
22	  );
23	  process.exit(1);
24	}
25	
26	const allowedOrigins = [
27	  "http://localhost:3000",
28	  // Covers all Vercel preview deployments for this project
29	  "https://*.vercel.app",
30	  ...(PRODUCTION_URL ? [PRODUCTION_URL] : []),
31	];
32	
33	const client = new S3Client({
34	  region: "auto",
35	  endpoint: `https://${R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,
36	  credentials: {
37	    accessKeyId: R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID,
38	    secretAccessKey: R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY,
39	  },
40	});
41	
42	console.log(`Applying CORS to bucket: ${R2_BUCKET}`);
43	console.log("Allowed origins:", allowedOrigins);
44	
45	await client.send(
46	  new PutBucketCorsCommand({
47	    Bucket: R2_BUCKET,
48	    CORSConfiguration: {
49	      CORSRules: [
50	        {
51	          // Browser PUT for bundle uploads (upload wizard → R2 presigned URL)
52	          AllowedOrigins: allowedOrigins,
53	          AllowedMethods: ["PUT"],
54	          AllowedHeaders: ["Content-Type", "Content-Length", "Content-MD5"],
55	          ExposeHeaders: ["ETag"],
56	          MaxAgeSeconds: 3600,
57	        },
58	        {
59	          // Public GET for any future direct-CDN reads (optional, safe to keep)
60	          AllowedOrigins: ["*"],
61	          AllowedMethods: ["GET", "HEAD"],
62	          AllowedHeaders: [],
63	          MaxAgeSeconds: 86400,
64	        },
65	      ],
66	    },
67	  }),
68	);
69	
70	// Read back to confirm
71	const { CORSRules } = await client.send(
72	  new GetBucketCorsCommand({ Bucket: R2_BUCKET }),
73	);
74	
75	console.log("\nCORS rules now in effect:");
76	console.log(JSON.stringify(CORSRules, null, 2));

> AGENT

Found it — but the issue isn't CORS, it's that **R2 isn't configured at all** on your machine. `.env.local` has zero `R2_*` vars. The chain that made it look like a fetch error:

1. `process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID ?? ""` defaults to empty string
2. Presign builds endpoint `https://.r2.cloudflarestorage.com` (empty subdomain) and produces a syntactically-valid-but-DNS-unresolvable URL
3. Browser PUT to that URL → DNS failure → `TypeError: Failed to fetch`

**For you right now:** you need to set up R2 to actually upload. Steps:

1. Create a Cloudflare R2 bucket (default name: `agentcenter-bundles`).
2. Create an R2 API token with read+write on that bucket.
3. Add to `.env.local`:
   ```
   R2_ACCOUNT_ID="..."
   R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID="..."
   R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY="..."
   R2_BUCKET="agentcenter-bundles"
   ```
4. Run `bun run r2:cors` to apply browser CORS rules.

**For the codebase:** the silent `?? ""` fallback is the real landmine — same pattern we just fixed for `DATABASE_URL`. Let me add the same fail-fast guard to R2 so the next person hits a useful "R2 not configured" message instead of a misleading `Failed to fetch`.

  "new_string": "import { S3Client, PutObjectCommand } from \"@aws-sdk/client-s3\";\nimport { getSignedUrl } from \"@aws-sdk/s3-request-presigner\";\n\nconst R2_BUCKET = process.env.R2_BUCKET ?? \"agentcenter-bundles\";\n\n// Validated on first use rather than at import time, so unrelated tests /\n// build steps that import this module without ever calling presign don't\n// blow up on missing creds.\nfunction getR2Config(): {\n  accountId: string;\n  accessKeyId: string;\n  secretAccessKey: string;\n} {\n  const accountId = process.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID;\n  const accessKeyId = process.env.R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID;\n  const secretAccessKey = process.env.R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY;\n  const missing = [\n    !accountId && \"R2_ACCOUNT_ID\",\n    !accessKeyId && \"R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\",\n    !secretAccessKey && \"R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\",\n  ].filter(Boolean);\n  if (missing.length > 0) {\n    // Failing here surfaces a clear message via the upload-sign route's\n    // catch block instead of an empty-subdomain URL that DNS-fails in the\n    // browser as `TypeError: Failed to fetch`.\n    throw new Error(\n      `R2 is not configured — missing env vars: ${missing.join(\", \")}. ` +\n        `See .env.example.`,\n    );\n  }\n  return {\n    accountId: accountId as string,\n    accessKeyId: accessKeyId as string,\n    secretAccessKey: secretAccessKey as string,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction getClient(): S3Client {\n  const { accountId, accessKeyId, secretAccessKey } = getR2Config();\n  return new S3Client({\n    region: \"auto\",\n    endpoint: `https://${accountId}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com`,\n    credentials: { accessKeyId, secretAccessKey },\n  });\n}"
}

> AGENT

403 `AccessDenied` — your R2 token has Object-level permissions, not bucket-admin (which is needed to set CORS). Two paths:

**Easiest: paste the CORS rules into the Cloudflare dashboard** (Bucket → Settings → CORS policy → Edit):

```json
[
  {
    "AllowedOrigins": ["http://localhost:3000"],
    "AllowedMethods": ["PUT"],
    "AllowedHeaders": ["Content-Type", "Content-Length", "Content-MD5"],
    "ExposeHeaders": ["ETag"],
    "MaxAgeSeconds": 3600
  },
  {
    "AllowedOrigins": ["*"],
    "AllowedMethods": ["GET", "HEAD"],
    "MaxAgeSeconds": 86400
  }
]
```

**Or:** create a temporary "Admin Read & Write" R2 token, swap your `.env.local` creds, run `bun run r2:cors`, swap back. More steps; same outcome.

Once CORS is applied, reload dev and Step 2 should work. Let me know when it does and I'll push the local branch + open a PR for the diagnostic + R2 fail-fast commits.

> AGENT

Two-part fix. **For you right now:** in a separate terminal, run `npx inngest-cli@latest dev`. That spins up Inngest's local dev server which doesn't need an event key — you can leave it running alongside `bun dev`.

**For the code:** if `inngest.send` fails, the version is currently stuck in `scanning` status with no scan job ever queued — that's a real bug, not just bad UX. Let me wrap the send, roll the status back to `pending` on failure, and surface a clear error.

If this fails (e.g. INNGEST_EVENT_KEY missing,\n  // Inngest unreachable), roll the version status back to `pending` so it\n  // doesn't sit stuck in `scanning` with no scan ever happening.\n  try {\n    const { inngest } = await import(\"@/lib/jobs/client\");\n    await inngest.send({\n      name: \"extension/scan.requested\",\n      data: { versionId, fileId: version.bundleFileId },\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[publish] inngest.send failed\", err);\n    try {\n      await db\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ status: \"pending\" })\n        .where(\n          and(\n            eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),\n            eq(extensionVersions.status, \"scanning\"),\n          ),\n        );\n    } catch (rollbackErr) {\n      console.error(\n        \"[publish] failed to roll back scanning -> pending after inngest error\",\n        rollbackErr,\n      );\n    }\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: \"scan_queue_unavailable\",\n      detail: devErrorDetail(err),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}"
}

> AGENT

The version is stuck in `scanning` from a prior failed attempt (before the rollback fix shipped). Two improvements:

1. **Make `submit()` idempotent**: allow retry from `scanning` (no-op transition, lets the inngest retry happen). The current `pending`-only check makes recovery impossible.
2. **Detect `VersionStateError` specifically** in the action and surface a useful message instead of `db_error`.

  "old_string": "// Move a version from pending to scanning. Caller is responsible for\n// kicking off the scan job (Inngest event) — this module owns DB state.\nexport async function submit(versionId: string): Promise<void> {\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(extensionVersions)\n    .set({ status: \"scanning\" })\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),\n        eq(extensionVersions.status, \"pending\"),\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: extensionVersions.id });\n  if (updated.length === 0) throw new VersionStateError(\"version_not_found\");\n}",
  "new_string": "// Move a version to scanning. Allowed source states are `pending` (the\n// normal first submit) and `scanning` itself (an idempotent retry, e.g.\n// when a previous attempt's Inngest send failed and left the row stuck —\n// without this the user would have no way to recover). Versions already\n// in `ready` or `rejected` reject the transition. Caller is responsible\n// for kicking off the scan job (Inngest event) — this module owns DB state.\nexport async function submit(versionId: string): Promise<void> {\n  const updated = await db\n    .update(extensionVersions)\n    .set({ status: \"scanning\" })\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId),\n        inArray(extensionVersions.status, [\"pending\", \"scanning\"]),\n      ),\n    )\n    .returning({ id: extensionVersions.id });\n  if (updated.length === 0) throw new VersionStateError(\"version_not_found\");\n}"
}

Clean. Two things needed for it to actually work end-to-end:

1. **Start the Inngest dev server** in a separate terminal:
   ```
   npx inngest-cli@latest dev
   ```
   It serves on `http://localhost:8288`. Leave it running.

2. **Restart your `bun dev`** so the Inngest client picks up the new `isDev: true` config.

Then retry submit. The version-not-submittable retry path is also now in place, so even if it had stuck again, you'd still recover.

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"fix(publish): unblock Step 2 + Step 3 error paths\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nStep 2 (upload) was failing as `Something went wrong. Please try again.` and Step 3 (submit for review) was failing as `We couldn't save your extension right now.` — both the wizard's catch-all fallback. The underlying causes were a chain of silent misconfigurations, opaque error wrapping, and one real correctness bug.\n\n### What landed\n\n- **8c707af** — Surface caught client-side errors in dev. The wizard's `try/catch` blocks now show `err.message` under the friendly headline (dev only), so a thrown exception lands in the red box instead of vanishing into the browser console.\n- **ff61ac2** — Fail clearly when R2 is not configured. `lib/storage/r2.ts` was masking missing creds with `?? \"\"`, producing a syntactically-valid presigned URL with an empty subdomain that DNS-failed in the browser as `TypeError: Failed to fetch`. Now validates `R2_ACCOUNT_ID` / `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID` / `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` on first use and throws a message naming the missing vars; the upload-sign route passes the message through (in dev) as a `detail` on the 503.\n- **18e2f9c** — Roll back `scanning` status if scan-queue send fails. The `submit()` DB transition committed before `inngest.send`; if the send threw (missing key, dev server down), the version was left stuck in `scanning` with no scan ever queued. New code wraps the send, attempts a best-effort `scanning -> pending` rollback, and returns `scan_queue_unavailable` with detail.\n- **3cc971b** — Allow submit retry from `scanning` + surface state errors distinctly. Before the rollback fix shipped, real users (myself) ended up with stuck `scanning` rows and *no way to recover* — `submit()` only allowed `pending -> scanning`. Broadened the source-state predicate to `pending` OR `scanning` (idempotent retry); `ready` / `rejected` still reject. The action now detects `VersionStateError` and returns `version_not_submittable` instead of mislabeling it as `db_error`.\n- **57b662c** — Enable Inngest dev mode locally. Default `new Inngest()` requires `INNGEST_EVENT_KEY` and routes to api.inngest.com — both wrong for local dev. `isDev: process.env.NODE_ENV !== \"production\"` routes to the local dev server (started via `npx inngest-cli@latest dev`) and waives the key requirement. Production behavior unchanged.\n\n### New error codes added (with EN + ZH messages)\n\n- `scan_queue_unavailable` — Inngest send failed; status was rolled back.\n- `version_not_submittable` — version isn't in a submittable state (already reviewed/published).\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] Without R2 env vars set, Step 2 shows \"Couldn't get an upload URL.\" with detail naming the missing vars.\n- [ ] With R2 + CORS configured, Step 2 upload succeeds.\n- [ ] Without `npx inngest-cli@latest dev` running, Step 3 shows \"Couldn't queue the bundle scan…\" and the version remains in `pending` (recoverable on retry).\n- [ ] With Inngest dev server running, Step 3 succeeds end-to-end.\n- [ ] Stale `scanning` row (from before the rollback fix) can be re-submitted without manual DB cleanup.\n- [ ] `bun run typecheck` / `bun run lint` / `bun run test` all pass.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Open pull request"
}
```

<details>
<summary>📝 Walkthrough</summary>

## Walkthrough

This PR strengthens error handling and resilience across the publish/upload pipeline. It introduces lazy validation of R2 credentials to avoid startup failures, configures Inngest for development, makes version scanning idempotent via state machine improvements, adds typed error handling in the submit action with automatic rollback on scan queueing failures, implements presign response detail parsing on the client, and adds new localized error messages for development and production visibility.

## Changes

**Publish Flow Error Handling and Resilience**

|Layer / File(s)|Summary|
|---|---|
|**Storage and Job Configuration** <br> `lib/storage/r2.ts`, `lib/jobs/client.ts`|R2 credentials are validated lazily at presign time instead of module scope; Inngest client adds `isDev` flag for local dev server routing when `NODE_ENV` is not `production`.|
|**State Machine Idempotent Retry** <br> `lib/extensions/state.ts`|`submit(versionId)` now accepts `pending` or `scanning` status, enabling safe retry when a scan is already in progress.|
|**Publish Action Error Handling** <br> `lib/actions/publish.ts`|New catch for `VersionStateError` returns `version_not_submittable`; Inngest scan dispatch wrapped in try/catch with automatic database rollback from `scanning` to `pending` on send failure, returning `scan_queue_unavailable`.|
|**Presign Endpoint Error Response** <br> `app/api/upload/sign/route.ts`|Error responses include structured JSON with conditional `detail` field (populated in dev, omitted in production).|
|**Client Error Capture and Display** <br> `components/publish/upload-wizard.tsx`|New `clientErrorDetail()` helper mirrors server-side dev check; presign error responses are parsed for optional JSON `detail` field; all exception handlers now set `errorDetail` instead of clearing it; `KNOWN_ERROR_CODES` extended for new server error codes.|
|**Localization** <br> `lib/i18n/messages/en.json`, `lib/i18n/messages/zh.json`|Added `publish.errors.version_not_submittable` and `publish.errors.scan_queue_unavailable` in English and Chinese.|

## Estimated code review effort

🎯 3 (Moderate) | ⏱️ ~20 minutes

## Possibly related PRs

- [ainaive/agentcenter#19](https://github.com/ainaive/agentcenter/pull/19): Modifies the same publish flow pieces (UploadWizard error detail handling and server-side publish/submit actions).

## Poem

> 🐰 *A rabbit's ode to resilient uploads:*
> 
> When storage credentials hide away,
> We fetch them when we need to play.
> Scanning retries, errors caught with care—
> Development sees all, production fair.
> From server down to client's hand,
> Now errors flow throughout the land! 🚀

</details>

<!-- walkthrough_end -->

> AGENT

Confirmed — `app/[locale]/publish/page.tsx` lists each draft as plain text + status badge, no link, no actions. There's no `/publish/[id]/edit` route to resume the wizard mid-flow either, so a user with a half-finished draft is stuck.

**Recommendation:** make each row a link to a new `/publish/[id]/edit` route that loads the draft + its version state, picks the right starting step (Step 2 if no bundle uploaded, Step 3 if bundle uploaded but not submitted), and reuses the existing `UploadWizard`. That requires the wizard to accept `initialDraft` + `initialStep` props, plus a small `getDraft(extensionId)` server action that loads the manifest fields back into form-shape.

**Main tradeoff:** ~100-150 lines of work. Smaller alternatives — "Discard draft" button only, or just surface the version status on the dashboard so users at least understand *why* they're stuck — would unblock without enabling resume. If you'd prefer the smaller scope first, say so.

What direction do you want to go?

> DEVELOPER

I think we can go with the all steps of recommendation, commit each steps, what do you think?

> DEVELOPER

I think not only discard draft, what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed — resume is the right answer. One material decision I want clarity on first:

**Plan A (simpler, ~120 lines):** Resume continues from where you left off. Step 1 fields shown read-only at the top of the wizard; user proceeds to Step 2 or Step 3 based on version state. No editing of an existing draft's manifest. To change Step 1 details, user discards + recreates.

**Plan B (~200 lines):** Step 1 is fully editable on resume. Adds `updateDraftExtension(extensionId, values)` action that updates the existing extension/version rows instead of inserting new ones. Better mental model but more code to write and test.

Plan A handles the most common case (closed tab mid-upload) cleanly. Plan B handles "oh, the description was wrong" without forcing a redo. Which one do you want?

> DEVELOPER

what's your opinion? it looks like plan b is better.

> AGENT

Honest opinion: **Plan A first**, despite your instinct toward B. Three reasons:

1. **The common case is "closed tab mid-upload"**, not "I want to edit details." For an unpublished draft, edit-via-discard-and-recreate is cheap (no audience, no installs, slug isn't reserved publicly).

2. **Plan B has subtle correctness traps** that Plan A sidesteps: changing `slug` orphans the R2 bundle (keyed by `slug/version`); editing while status is `scanning`/`ready` opens a state-machine question (block, or allow + re-scan?); tag updates need delete-then-insert. Each of those is a small decision but they multiply.

3. **Plan B is additive on top of A**. If real users complain "I just want to fix a typo in the description," we can add `updateDraftExtension` later with the right state-aware semantics. Hard to undo if we ship it half-baked.

If you push for B I'll do it — but I'd ship A first and earn the right to add B based on actual feedback.

> DEVELOPER

ok, go ahead

> AGENT

Starting Plan A. First, branch and quick survey of related routes.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {\n  return db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(extensions.createdAt);\n}\n\nexport async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  return row?.status ?? null;\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {\n  // Pull the latest version per extension alongside the extension row so the\n  // dashboard can show \"where am I in the wizard\" (e.g. needs upload vs.\n  // submitted, awaiting scan) — visibility alone hides that info.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));\n\n  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the\n  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per\n  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.\n  const byExtension = new Map<string, (typeof rows)[number]>();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) byExtension.set(row.id, row);\n  }\n  return Array.from(byExtension.values());\n}\n\nexport async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({ status: extensionVersions.status })\n    .from(extensionVersions)\n    .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, versionId))\n    .limit(1);\n  return row?.status ?? null;\n}\n\nexport type DraftSnapshot = {\n  extensionId: string;\n  versionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  version: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  visibility: string;\n  versionStatus: string;\n  bundleUploaded: boolean;\n};\n\nexport type GetDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true; draft: DraftSnapshot }\n  | { ok: false; error: \"not_found\" | \"not_owner\" | \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n// Load an extension + its latest version for the publish wizard's resume\n// flow. Owner-checked: callers cannot read another user's draft. Returns\n// just enough state for the wizard to land on the right step and render\n// a read-only manifest summary.\nexport async function getDraft(extensionId: string): Promise<GetDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId || !row.version || !row.versionStatus) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_owner\" };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    draft: {\n      extensionId: row.extensionId,\n      versionId: row.versionId,\n      slug: row.slug,\n      version: row.version,\n      name: row.name,\n      category: row.category,\n      visibility: row.visibility,\n      versionStatus: row.versionStatus,\n      bundleUploaded: row.bundleFileId !== null,\n    },\n  };\n}"
}

68	  // Translate a server-action error code into a localized message. Unknown
69	  // codes fall back to a generic message rather than leaking raw codes (e.g.
70	  // "db_error") to the user.
71	  function describeError(code: string): string {
72	    return KNOWN_ERROR_CODES.has(code) ? te(code) : te("fallback");
73	  }
74	
75	  // Reset the file-upload state. Called after a fresh draft is created so
76	  // a re-edited Step 1 doesn't leave the wizard thinking the previous
77	  // bundle is still attached to the new versionId.
78	  function resetUploadState() {
79	    setFileUploaded(false);
80	    setUploadProgress("idle");
81	    if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
82	  }
83	
84	  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {
85	    clearError();
86	    try {
87	      const result = await createDraftExtension(values);
88	      if (!result.ok) {
89	        setError(describeError(result.error));
90	        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);
91	        return;
92	      }
93	      setDraft({
94	        extensionId: result.extensionId,
95	        versionId: result.versionId,
96	        slug: values.slug,
97	        version: values.version,
98	      });
99	      resetUploadState();
100	      setStep(2);
101	    } catch (err) {
102	      console.error("[publish] handleManifestSubmit threw", err);
103	      setError(te("fallback"));
104	      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));
105	    }
106	  }
107

  "new_string": "// Subset of `DraftSnapshot` from the resume server action — kept here as a\n// structural type so this component doesn't import server-only modules.\nexport interface ResumeDraft {\n  extensionId: string;\n  versionId: string;\n  slug: string;\n  version: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string;\n  bundleUploaded: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface UploadWizardProps {\n  // When set, the wizard runs in \"resume\" mode: Step 1 is skipped, the\n  // manifest details are shown read-only at the top of every step, and the\n  // initial step is derived from whether the bundle is already uploaded.\n  resume?: ResumeDraft;\n}\n\nexport function UploadWizard({ resume }: UploadWizardProps = {}) {\n  const tw = useTranslations(\"publish.wizard\");\n  const tu = useTranslations(\"publish.upload\");\n  const te = useTranslations(\"publish.errors\");\n\n  const initialStep: Step = resume ? (resume.bundleUploaded ? 3 : 2) : 1;\n\n  const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(initialStep);\n  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<DraftState | null>(\n    resume\n      ? {\n          extensionId: resume.extensionId,\n          versionId: resume.versionId,\n          slug: resume.slug,\n          version: resume.version,\n        }\n      : null,\n  );\n  const [fileUploaded, setFileUploaded] = useState(resume?.bundleUploaded ?? false);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [errorDetail, setErrorDetail] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [uploadProgress, setUploadProgress] = useState<\"idle\" | \"uploading\" | \"done\">(\n    resume?.bundleUploaded ? \"done\" : \"idle\",\n  );\n  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const isResume = resume !== undefined;"
}

  "content": "import { notFound, redirect } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { getTranslations } from \"next-intl/server\";\n\nimport { getSession } from \"@/lib/auth/session\";\nimport { getDraft } from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\nimport { UploadWizard } from \"@/components/publish/upload-wizard\";\n\nexport async function generateMetadata() {\n  const t = await getTranslations(\"publish.wizard\");\n  return { title: t(\"resumingLabel\") };\n}\n\ninterface PageProps {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string; id: string }>;\n}\n\nexport default async function PublishEditPage({ params }: PageProps) {\n  const session = await getSession();\n  if (!session) redirect(\"/sign-in\");\n\n  const { id } = await params;\n  const result = await getDraft(id);\n  if (!result.ok) {\n    // Both `not_found` and `not_owner` collapse to a 404 — we don't want\n    // to leak whether an extension exists for someone else's account.\n    notFound();\n  }\n\n  const { draft } = result;\n\n  // Resume only makes sense for in-flight versions. If the version has\n  // already been scanned/rejected/published, the user has nothing to do\n  // here — bounce them back to the dashboard.\n  if (draft.versionStatus !== \"pending\") {\n    redirect(\"/publish\");\n  }\n\n  const t = await getTranslations(\"publish.wizard\");\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10\">\n      <h1 className=\"font-display text-2xl font-semibold mb-8\">\n        {t(\"resumingLabel\")}\n      </h1>\n      <UploadWizard\n        resume={{\n          extensionId: draft.extensionId,\n          versionId: draft.versionId,\n          slug: draft.slug,\n          version: draft.version,\n          name: draft.name,\n          category: draft.category,\n          bundleUploaded: draft.bundleUploaded,\n        }}\n      />\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n"
}

Resumable rows link into the wizard; everything\n// else shows status only.\nfunction rowAction(\n  status: string | null,\n  bundleUploaded: boolean,\n): \"resume_upload\" | \"resume_submit\" | \"scanning\" | \"ready\" | \"rejected\" | \"none\" {\n  if (status === \"pending\") {\n    return bundleUploaded ? \"resume_submit\" : \"resume_upload\";\n  }\n  if (status === \"scanning\") return \"scanning\";\n  if (status === \"ready\") return \"ready\";\n  if (status === \"rejected\") return \"rejected\";\n  return \"none\";\n}\n\nexport default async function PublishDashboardPage() {\n  const session = await getSession();\n  if (!session) redirect(\"/sign-in\");\n\n  const t = await getTranslations(\"publish.dashboard\");\n  const exts = await getMyExtensions(session.user.id);\n\n  const visibilityLabel: Record<string, string> = {\n    draft: t(\"statusDraft\"),\n    published: t(\"statusPublished\"),\n    archived: t(\"statusArchived\"),\n  };\n\n  const versionStatusLabel: Record<string, string> = {\n    resume_upload: t(\"versionPendingUpload\"),\n    resume_submit: t(\"versionReadyToSubmit\"),\n    scanning: t(\"versionScanning\"),\n    ready: t(\"versionReady\"),\n    rejected: t(\"versionRejected\"),\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-8\">\n        <h1 className=\"font-display text-2xl font-semibold\">{t(\"title\")}</h1>\n        <Link\n          href=\"/publish/new\"\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 transition-opacity\"\n        >\n          <Plus className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n          {t(\"newButton\")}\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n\n      {exts.length === 0 ? (\n        <div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed p-12 text-center\">\n          <Package className=\"text-muted-foreground/50 size-9\" />\n          <h2 className=\"text-foreground text-base font-semibold\">\n            {t(\"emptyTitle\")}\n          </h2>\n          <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-xs text-[13px] leading-relaxed\">\n            {t(\"empty\")}\n          </p>\n          <Link\n            href=\"/publish/new\"\n            className=\"bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-opacity\"\n          >\n            <Plus className=\"size-3.5\" />\n            {t(\"emptyCta\")}\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <ul className=\"space-y-3\">\n          {exts.map((ext) => {\n            const action = rowAction(ext.latestStatus, ext.latestBundleFileId !== null);\n            const resumable = action === \"resume_upload\" || action === \"resume_submit\";\n            const visibility = visibilityLabel[ext.visibility] ?? ext.visibility;\n            const versionLabel =\n              action !== \"none\" ? versionStatusLabel[action] : null;\n\n            const body = (\n              <>\n                <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1 truncate\">\n                  <span className=\"font-medium\">{ext.name}</span>\n                  <span className=\"ml-2 font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                    {ext.slug}\n                  </span>\n                  {versionLabel && (\n                    <span className=\"ml-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                      · {versionLabel}\n                    </span>\n                  )}\n                </div>\n                <span className=\"shrink-0 rounded-full border border-border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                  {visibility}\n                </span>\n              </>\n            );\n\n            return (\n              <li key={ext.id}>\n                {resumable ? (\n                  <Link\n                    href={`/publish/${ext.id}/edit`}\n                    className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-5 py-4 transition-colors hover:border-primary\"\n                  >\n                    {body}\n                  </Link>\n                ) : (\n                  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-border bg-card px-5 py-4\">\n                    {body}\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </li>\n            );\n          })}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n"
}

92	  );

95

100	
101	  // Translate a server-action error code into a localized message. Unknown
102	  // codes fall back to a generic message rather than leaking raw codes (e.g.
103	  // "db_error") to the user.
104	  function describeError(code: string): string {

- `lib/actions/publish.ts` — `getDraft(extensionId)` (owner-checked) + `getMyExtensions` now returns latest version status + bundle presence.
- `app/[locale]/publish/[id]/edit/page.tsx` — new resume route. 404 on not-found / not-owner. Redirects to dashboard if version isn't `pending` (already submitted or beyond).

- `app/[locale]/publish/page.tsx` — rows for resumable drafts are now links; rows show version-stage label ("Needs bundle upload" / "Ready to submit" / "Awaiting scan" / etc.).

> AGENT

CodeRabbit posted 1 outside-diff issue + 1 nitpick — both legit:

**1. Step 3 summary has hardcoded English** ("Slug", "Version", "Bundle", "✓ Uploaded") in `upload-wizard.tsx:353-365`. Pre-existing bug, made more visible by resume mode (non-English users now land directly on Step 3). Worth fixing.

**2. `getDraft` returns `not_owner` distinctly from `not_found`** — page already collapses both to 404, so having a distinct code in the public contract risks a future caller branching on it and leaking existence. Tighten the union.

Both green-light fixes. Doing them now.

Owner-checked: callers cannot read another user's draft. Returns\n// just enough state for the wizard to land on the right step and render\n// a read-only manifest summary.\nexport async function getDraft(extensionId: string): Promise<GetDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId || !row.version || !row.versionStatus) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_owner\" };\n  }",
  "new_string": "export type GetDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true; draft: DraftSnapshot }\n  // Deliberately collapse non-owner to `not_found` so the public contract\n  // can't be used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else's\n  // account.\n  | { ok: false; error: \"not_found\" | \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n// Load an extension + its latest version for the publish wizard's resume\n// flow. Owner-checked: callers cannot read another user's draft. Returns\n// just enough state for the wizard to land on the right step and render\n// a read-only manifest summary.\nexport async function getDraft(extensionId: string): Promise<GetDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      extensionId: extensions.id,\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      version: extensionVersions.version,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      bundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId || !row.version || !row.versionStatus) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    // Same shape as a missing row; see comment on `GetDraftResult`.\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }"
}

> AGENT

Looking at `docs/plan.md` for context: we're effectively at the tail end of Phase 10 (upload wizard) with bits of Phase 11/12 already in place. Most natural next moves, ranked:

1. **Discard draft button** (~50 lines) — pairs with resume to give the dashboard a complete interaction model. Right now there's no way to clean up an abandoned draft, so they pile up forever and stuck-state versions need DB surgery. Smallest bang-per-buck win.
2. **Tests for `getDraft` + wizard resume mode** — pure defensive. The owner-collapse-to-`not_found` and status-routing logic can silently regress without coverage.
3. **Phase 13 polish** (per plan: `error.tsx`, `loading.tsx`, empty states, OG images, a11y) — bigger, but well-scoped and the last lap before v1.
4. **Plan B (edit existing draft manifest)** — defer until real users actually complain.

My recommendation: **(1) Discard draft now**, then either (2) tests or (3) polish depending on appetite. Which way?

> DEVELOPER

let's do all of these, what do you think?

> AGENT

Agreed they all earn their place — but bundling all four into one PR would be ~800+ lines spanning four unrelated concerns, hard to review and easy to regress. I'd ship them as a sequence:

1. **PR A:** Discard draft button + tests for `getDraft`/resume routing. (Both small, both focused on the publish dashboard surface — naturally pair.)
2. **PR B:** Plan B — `updateDraftExtension` action + editable Step 1 in resume mode. Distinct concern, touches the wizard heavily.
3. **PR C... :** Phase 13 polish, broken into per-surface PRs (error.tsx + loading.tsx, empty states, OG images, a11y). Polish across many files in one PR is review-hostile.

Each merges before the next starts so we don't stack rebases. Sound good? Starting with PR A unless you redirect.

> DEVELOPER

can we make one PR 3 commits?

> AGENT

Yes — but I'd narrow it to **two commits in one PR**, not three. Here's why:

1. **Discard + tests** (commit 1) and **Plan B / editable manifest** (commit 2) both finish the "publish wizard correctness" arc — they share files, share mental model, share the same review focus. Easy to bundle.

2. **Phase 13 polish** is a different phase, touches surfaces unrelated to publish (home, browse, detail, sign-in), and is itself five workstreams (error.tsx, loading.tsx, empty states, OG images, a11y). Wedging that into the same PR widens review surface across the entire app. Even reviewer attention budgets break down — CodeRabbit does best when a PR has one theme.

So my proposal: **one PR, two commits** (publish-completeness arc), then start polish as its own PR sequence. If you really want three commits in one PR, the third commit should be a *narrow* slice of polish — e.g. just `loading.tsx` + `error.tsx` for `/publish/*` routes, not the full Phase 13 grab bag. Which way?

> DEVELOPER

ok, one PR, two or more commits

26	// Coerce empty / missing optional strings to `null`. We can't use `?? null`
27	// (it keeps `""`, which then FK-violates against `departments.id`) and we
28	// avoid `|| null` because it would also drop a legitimate `"0"`.
29	function emptyToNull(v: string | undefined | null): string | null {
30	  return v == null || v === "" ? null : v;

  "old_string": "export async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {",
  "new_string": "export type DiscardDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; error: \"not_found\" | \"unauthenticated\" | \"not_discardable\" };\n\n// Permanently delete a draft extension. Owner-checked. Only allowed while\n// visibility === \"draft\" — published or archived extensions are not\n// throwaway. Cascading FK constraints clean up extension_versions,\n// extension_tags, and (via versions) any associated files row.\n//\n// R2 bundle objects are intentionally left in place; orphan cleanup is\n// the bucket lifecycle's job, not the user's.\nexport async function discardDraft(\n  extensionId: string,\n): Promise<DiscardDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  // Treat not-found and not-owner identically so the contract can't be\n  // used to probe whether an extension exists for someone else.\n  if (!row || row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.visibility !== \"draft\") {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_discardable\" };\n  }\n\n  await db.delete(extensions).where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport async function getVersionStatus(versionId: string) {"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/components/publish/discard-button.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState, useTransition } from \"react\";\nimport { useRouter } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { Trash2 } from \"lucide-react\";\n\nimport { discardDraft } from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\n\ninterface Props {\n  extensionId: string;\n  extensionName: string;\n}\n\n// \"Discard\" affordance for a draft row on the publish dashboard. Confirms\n// via `window.confirm` rather than a custom dialog — the surface is\n// owner-only, the action is reversible only by re-creating, and we don't\n// want to pull in a new shadcn component for one button.\nexport function DiscardButton({ extensionId, extensionName }: Props) {\n  const td = useTranslations(\"publish.discard\");\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  function onClick() {\n    if (!confirm(td(\"confirm\", { name: extensionName }))) return;\n    startTransition(async () => {\n      setError(null);\n      try {\n        const result = await discardDraft(extensionId);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          setError(td(`error_${result.error}`));\n          return;\n        }\n        // Server-side `getMyExtensions` won't pick up the deletion until\n        // the route revalidates. `router.refresh()` re-runs the server\n        // component without a full reload.\n        router.refresh();\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error(\"[publish] discardDraft threw\", err);\n        setError(td(\"error_fallback\"));\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-end gap-1\">\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        onClick={onClick}\n        disabled={pending}\n        aria-label={td(\"ariaLabel\", { name: extensionName })}\n        className=\"inline-flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted hover:text-destructive disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors\"\n      >\n        <Trash2 className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n      </button>\n      {error && <span className=\"text-xs text-destructive\">{error}</span>}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "command": "git add -A && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(publish): discard a draft from the dashboard\n\nResume gave users a way to continue an in-flight draft, but no way to\nabandon one. Stuck drafts (e.g. wrong slug, version stuck mid-state)\nrequired manual DB cleanup.\n\n- `discardDraft(extensionId)` server action. Owner-checked; non-owner\n  collapses to `not_found` for the same anti-probing reason as\n  `getDraft`. Refuses to delete `published` / `archived` extensions\n  (returns `not_discardable`). Cascading FK constraints clean up\n  versions / tags / files-row links automatically. R2 bundle objects\n  are intentionally left for bucket lifecycle to clean up — orphan\n  cleanup isn't the user's job.\n- `<DiscardButton>` client component on the dashboard. Native\n  `window.confirm` for confirmation (owner-only surface, not worth\n  pulling in a new shadcn dialog component for one button); calls\n  `router.refresh()` on success so the dashboard re-renders without a\n  full reload.\n- Dashboard layout: each `draft` row gets a trash button to the right\n  of the body. Published/archived rows don't show it.\n- New `publish.discard.*` i18n keys (EN + ZH) covering aria label,\n  confirm prompt, and error messages.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit discard feature"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/publish/row-action.ts",
  "content": "// Decide what action the publish dashboard exposes for an extension based\n// on its latest version state. Pulled out as a pure function so the\n// state-routing logic (which step the resume link lands on, what status\n// label to show, whether the row is clickable) is unit-testable without\n// mounting the dashboard server component.\n//\n// `null` status covers the (currently unreachable, but defensive) case of\n// an extension with no versions — ROW_ACTIONS treats it the same as a\n// non-resumable row.\nexport type RowAction =\n  | \"resume_upload\" // pending, no bundle uploaded yet → land on Step 2\n  | \"resume_submit\" // pending, bundle uploaded but not submitted → Step 3\n  | \"scanning\" // submitted, bundle scan in flight\n  | \"ready\" // scan passed, awaiting publish\n  | \"rejected\" // scan failed\n  | \"none\"; // no action / unknown status\n\nexport function rowAction(\n  status: string | null,\n  bundleUploaded: boolean,\n): RowAction {\n  if (status === \"pending\") {\n    return bundleUploaded ? \"resume_submit\" : \"resume_upload\";\n  }\n  if (status === \"scanning\") return \"scanning\";\n  if (status === \"ready\") return \"ready\";\n  if (status === \"rejected\") return \"rejected\";\n  return \"none\";\n}\n\nexport function isResumable(action: RowAction): boolean {\n  return action === \"resume_upload\" || action === \"resume_submit\";\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/publish/row-action.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\nimport { isResumable, rowAction } from \"./row-action\";\n\ndescribe(\"rowAction\", () => {\n  it(\"routes pending without bundle to resume_upload (lands on Step 2)\", () => {\n    expect(rowAction(\"pending\", false)).toBe(\"resume_upload\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"routes pending with bundle to resume_submit (lands on Step 3)\", () => {\n    expect(rowAction(\"pending\", true)).toBe(\"resume_submit\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"maps scanning / ready / rejected to their own non-clickable rows\", () => {\n    expect(rowAction(\"scanning\", true)).toBe(\"scanning\");\n    expect(rowAction(\"ready\", true)).toBe(\"ready\");\n    expect(rowAction(\"rejected\", true)).toBe(\"rejected\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"treats null status (e.g. orphan extension with no versions) as 'none'\", () => {\n    expect(rowAction(null, false)).toBe(\"none\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"treats unknown future statuses as 'none' rather than crashing\", () => {\n    expect(rowAction(\"archived_internally\" as string, true)).toBe(\"none\");\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"isResumable\", () => {\n  it(\"only the two resume_* actions are resumable\", () => {\n    expect(isResumable(\"resume_upload\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(isResumable(\"resume_submit\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(isResumable(\"scanning\")).toBe(false);\n    expect(isResumable(\"ready\")).toBe(false);\n    expect(isResumable(\"rejected\")).toBe(false);\n    expect(isResumable(\"none\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish-ownership.test.ts",
  "content": "// Security-critical regression tests: `getDraft` and `discardDraft` must\n// treat a non-owner request identically to a missing row, so the public\n// contracts can't be used to probe for someone else's extensions.\n//\n// We don't aim to fully cover the actions here — the DB-touching paths\n// are best exercised end-to-end. These tests pin the ownership collapse\n// because it's exactly the kind of thing a refactor can silently undo.\n\nimport { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\n\nconst selectMock = vi.fn();\nconst deleteMock = vi.fn();\nconst getSessionMock = vi.fn();\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/db/client\", () => ({\n  db: {\n    select: (...a: unknown[]) => selectMock(...a),\n    delete: (...a: unknown[]) => deleteMock(...a),\n  },\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"@/lib/auth\", () => ({\n  auth: {\n    api: {\n      getSession: (...a: unknown[]) => getSessionMock(...a),\n    },\n  },\n}));\n\nvi.mock(\"next/headers\", () => ({\n  headers: () => Promise.resolve(new Headers()),\n}));\n\nimport { discardDraft, getDraft } from \"@/lib/actions/publish\";\n\n// Build a select chain that resolves to a single row (or empty) at .limit().\nfunction selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {\n  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);\n  const orderBy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });\n  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit, orderBy });\n  const leftJoin = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });\n  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where, leftJoin });\n  return { from, leftJoin, where, orderBy, limit };\n}\n\nfunction deleteChain() {\n  const where = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined);\n  return { where };\n}\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks();\n});\n\ndescribe(\"discardDraft\", () => {\n  it(\"returns unauthenticated when no session\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue(null);\n    const result = await discardDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" });\n    expect(selectMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns not_found for a missing extension\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(selectChain([]));\n    const result = await discardDraft(\"ext-missing\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_found\" });\n    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  // Regression: non-owner MUST collapse to `not_found`, not a distinct\n  // error — otherwise the contract leaks existence.\n  it(\"returns not_found (not 'not_owner') when a different user owns it\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([{ publisherUserId: \"user-B\", visibility: \"draft\" }]),\n    );\n    const result = await discardDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_found\" });\n    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"refuses to discard a published extension\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([{ publisherUserId: \"user-A\", visibility: \"published\" }]),\n    );\n    const result = await discardDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_discardable\" });\n    expect(deleteMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"deletes the extension when the caller is the owner of a draft\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([{ publisherUserId: \"user-A\", visibility: \"draft\" }]),\n    );\n    deleteMock.mockReturnValue(deleteChain());\n\n    const result = await discardDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: true });\n    expect(deleteMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"getDraft\", () => {\n  it(\"returns unauthenticated when no session\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue(null);\n    const result = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" });\n    expect(selectMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns not_found for a missing extension or one without a version\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(selectChain([]));\n    const empty = await getDraft(\"ext-missing\");\n    expect(empty).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_found\" });\n\n    // Extension exists but the leftJoin produced no version columns.\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          slug: \"x\",\n          name: \"X\",\n          category: \"skills\",\n          visibility: \"draft\",\n          versionId: null,\n          version: null,\n          versionStatus: null,\n          bundleFileId: null,\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    const noVersion = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(noVersion).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_found\" });\n  });\n\n  // Regression: non-owner MUST collapse to `not_found`. The public\n  // GetDraftResult union deliberately doesn't include `not_owner`.\n  it(\"returns not_found (not 'not_owner') for another user's extension\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n          publisherUserId: \"user-B\",\n          slug: \"x\",\n          name: \"X\",\n          category: \"skills\",\n          visibility: \"draft\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          version: \"1.0.0\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n          bundleFileId: null,\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    const result = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: false, error: \"not_found\" });\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns the DraftSnapshot for the owner\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          slug: \"my-skill\",\n          name: \"My Skill\",\n          category: \"skills\",\n          visibility: \"draft\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          version: \"1.0.0\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n          bundleFileId: \"f-1\", // bundle uploaded\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    const result = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result).toEqual({\n      ok: true,\n      draft: {\n        extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n        versionId: \"v-1\",\n        slug: \"my-skill\",\n        version: \"1.0.0\",\n        name: \"My Skill\",\n        category: \"skills\",\n        visibility: \"draft\",\n        versionStatus: \"pending\",\n        bundleUploaded: true,\n      },\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"reports bundleUploaded: false when bundleFileId is null\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          extensionId: \"ext-1\",\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          slug: \"my-skill\",\n          name: \"My Skill\",\n          category: \"skills\",\n          visibility: \"draft\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          version: \"1.0.0\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n          bundleFileId: null,\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    const result = await getDraft(\"ext-1\");\n    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);\n    if (result.ok) expect(result.draft.bundleUploaded).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"
}

Now Plan B. Plan:

2. Add `updateDraftExtension(extensionId, values)` server action — owner-checked, only allowed while version status is `pending`.

23	    out.push({ id: d.id, label: "  ".repeat(depth) + d.name });

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts",
  "old_string": "export type DiscardDraftResult =",
  "new_string": "export type UpdateDraftResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; error: string; detail?: string };\n\n// Update an in-flight draft's manifest fields. Owner-checked. Refuses to\n// touch anything once the version has left `pending` — by then the\n// scanner has likely picked it up and re-running scans is the worker's\n// job, not the wizard's. `slug` and `version` can technically be\n// changed, but the wizard locks them in resume mode because they form\n// the R2 bundle key — letting them shift orphans the uploaded bundle.\nexport async function updateDraftExtension(\n  extensionId: string,\n  raw: ManifestFormValues,\n): Promise<UpdateDraftResult> {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: await headers() });\n  if (!session) return { ok: false, error: \"unauthenticated\" };\n\n  const parsed = ManifestFormSchema.safeParse(raw);\n  if (!parsed.success) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      error: \"invalid_input\",\n      detail: parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join(\"; \"),\n    };\n  }\n  const data = parsed.data;\n\n  // Resolve the latest version + verify ownership + state in one read.\n  const [row] = await db\n    .select({\n      publisherUserId: extensions.publisherUserId,\n      versionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      versionStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensionVersions.createdAt))\n    .limit(1);\n\n  if (!row || !row.versionId) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.publisherUserId !== session.user.id) {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"not_found\" };\n  }\n  if (row.versionStatus !== \"pending\") {\n    return { ok: false, error: \"version_not_editable\" };\n  }\n\n  try {\n    await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n      await tx\n        .update(extensions)\n        .set({\n          slug: data.slug,\n          name: data.name,\n          nameZh: emptyToNull(data.nameZh),\n          tagline: emptyToNull(data.tagline),\n          description: emptyToNull(data.description),\n          descriptionZh: emptyToNull(data.descriptionZh),\n          category: data.category,\n          scope: data.scope,\n          funcCat: data.funcCat,\n          subCat: data.subCat,\n          l2: emptyToNull(data.l2),\n          deptId: emptyToNull(data.deptId),\n          homepageUrl: emptyToNull(data.homepageUrl),\n          repoUrl: emptyToNull(data.repoUrl),\n          licenseSpdx: emptyToNull(data.licenseSpdx),\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        })\n        .where(eq(extensions.id, extensionId));\n\n      await tx\n        .update(extensionVersions)\n        .set({ version: data.version })\n        .where(eq(extensionVersions.id, row.versionId as string));\n\n      // Tags: replace wholesale. The set is capped at 8 by the form\n      // schema, so a delete-then-insert is cheaper and clearer than\n      // computing a diff.\n      await tx\n        .delete(extensionTags)\n        .where(eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensionId));\n      if (data.tagIds.length > 0) {\n        await tx\n          .insert(extensionTags)\n          .values(data.tagIds.map((tagId) => ({ extensionId, tagId })));\n      }\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    const code = classifyDraftError(err);\n    console.error(\"[publish] updateDraftExtension failed\", {\n      code,\n      pgCode: pgErrorCode(err),\n      constraint: pgConstraint(err),\n      extensionId,\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n    return { ok: false, error: code, detail: devErrorDetail(err) };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nexport type DiscardDraftResult ="
}
```

  "new_string": "  async function handleManifestSubmit(values: ManifestFormValues) {\n    clearError();\n    try {\n      // Edit mode: update the existing extension + version in place.\n      // Slug and version are locked in the form so the bundle's R2 key\n      // remains valid; we don't reset upload state here.\n      if (resume) {\n        const result = await updateDraftExtension(resume.extensionId, values);\n        if (!result.ok) {\n          setError(describeError(result.error));\n          setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n        // Mirror the latest values into local draft state so Step 3's\n        // summary panel reflects what the user just saved.\n        setDraft({\n          extensionId: resume.extensionId,\n          versionId: resume.versionId,\n          slug: values.slug,\n          version: values.version,\n        });\n        setStep(2);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const result = await createDraftExtension(values);\n      if (!result.ok) {\n        setError(describeError(result.error));\n        setErrorDetail(result.detail ?? null);\n        return;\n      }\n      setDraft({\n        extensionId: result.extensionId,\n        versionId: result.versionId,\n        slug: values.slug,\n        version: values.version,\n      });\n      resetUploadState();\n      setStep(2);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(\"[publish] handleManifestSubmit threw\", err);\n      setError(te(\"fallback\"));\n      setErrorDetail(clientErrorDetail(err));\n    }\n  }"
}

  "old_string": "// Build a select chain that resolves to a single row (or empty) at .limit().\nfunction selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {\n  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);\n  const orderBy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });\n  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit, orderBy });\n  const leftJoin = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });\n  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where, leftJoin });\n  return { from, leftJoin, where, orderBy, limit };\n}",
  "new_string": "// Build a select chain whose `.where()` is BOTH thenable (for the simple\n// `select(...).from(t).where(...)` shape) AND continues into `.orderBy`/\n// `.limit` (for the joined-row shape). This lets the same mock cover\n// both query patterns getDraft uses.\nfunction selectChain(rows: unknown[]) {\n  const limit = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(rows);\n  const orderBy = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ limit });\n  const whereResult: {\n    limit: typeof limit;\n    orderBy: typeof orderBy;\n    then: Promise<unknown[]>[\"then\"];\n  } = {\n    limit,\n    orderBy,\n    then: ((onFulfilled, onRejected) =>\n      Promise.resolve(rows).then(\n        onFulfilled,\n        onRejected,\n      )) as Promise<unknown[]>[\"then\"],\n  };\n  const where = vi.fn().mockReturnValue(whereResult);\n  const leftJoin = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where });\n  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ where, leftJoin });\n  return { from, leftJoin, where, orderBy, limit };\n}"
}

{
  "command": "git add -A && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(publish): edit a draft's manifest fields on resume (Plan B)\n\nResume previously dropped the user back at Step 2 / Step 3 with no way\nto fix a typo in Step 1. They had to discard + recreate. Now Step 1 is\neditable in resume mode and writes through `updateDraftExtension`.\n\n- `updateDraftExtension(extensionId, values)` server action. Owner-\n  checked. Refuses when `version.status !== \"pending\"` (returns new\n  code `version_not_editable`) — once the scanner has the row, the\n  worker owns lifecycle, not the wizard. Writes are atomic via\n  `db.transaction`: extension row, version row, then a wholesale\n  delete+insert of the tag set (capped at 8 by the form schema, so a\n  diff-and-patch isn't worth the complexity).\n- `getDraft` now also returns the full `ManifestFormValues` (joined\n  manifest fields + a sibling query for `extension_tags`). Nullable\n  DB columns surface as empty strings so the controlled inputs don't\n  go uncontrolled.\n- `ManifestForm`: new `lockedFields` prop disables specific inputs.\n  `slug` and `version` are locked in resume mode because they form\n  the R2 bundle key (`bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip`); letting\n  them shift while a bundle is uploaded would orphan the bundle. New\n  `submitLabel` prop lets the wizard show \"Save & continue\" in edit\n  mode for clarity.\n- Wizard: in resume mode Step 1 is now reachable and pre-filled.\n  `handleManifestSubmit` branches on `resume` — calls\n  `updateDraftExtension` and skips the upload-state reset (since\n  slug + version are locked, the bundle stays valid). Step 2's Back\n  button walks back to Step 1 instead of linking to the dashboard;\n  the resume banner already carries the dashboard link.\n- New i18n: `wizard.saveAndContinue`, `form.lockedHint`,\n  `errors.version_not_editable` (EN + ZH).\n- publish-ownership tests updated for the second `select` call\n  against `extension_tags`. `selectChain` now produces a `where()`\n  result that's both thenable (no .limit) and chainable (.orderBy /\n  .limit), so the same helper covers both query shapes getDraft now\n  uses.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit Plan B"
}
