Native `window.confirm` for confirmation; cascading FKs handle versions/tags/files cleanup; R2 bundles intentionally left for bucket lifecycle. Non-owner collapses to `not_found` for the same anti-probing reason as `getDraft`.\n\n2. **8900f9b — Tests.** Extracts `rowAction(status, bundleUploaded)` from the dashboard page to `lib/publish/row-action.ts` and unit-tests it (6 cases). Adds `lib/actions/publish-ownership.test.ts` (10 cases) pinning the security contract: non-owner returns `not_found`, never a distinct `not_owner` code, on both `getDraft` and `discardDraft`. Also covers unauthenticated, missing row, missing version, and `not_discardable`.\n\n3. **3cd0294 — Plan B.** Step 1 is editable in resume mode now. New `updateDraftExtension(extensionId, values)` action: owner-checked, refuses when `version.status !== \"pending\"` (new error code `version_not_editable`), writes are atomic via `db.transaction` with a wholesale delete+insert of the tag set. `getDraft` extended to return the full `ManifestFormValues` (joined manifest fields + sibling `extension_tags` query). `ManifestForm` gets `lockedFields` and `submitLabel` props — the wizard locks `slug` and `version` in resume mode since they form the R2 bundle key (`bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip`); letting them shift while a bundle is uploaded would orphan the bundle.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] Dashboard row for a `pending` draft with no bundle → \"Needs bundle upload\" label, clickable, lands on Step 2.\n- [ ] Dashboard row for a `pending` draft with bundle → \"Ready to submit\", clickable, lands on Step 3.\n- [ ] Dashboard row for `scanning` / `ready` / `rejected` → non-clickable, correct stage label.\n- [ ] Trash icon on a draft row prompts native confirm; on confirm the row disappears via `router.refresh()`.\n- [ ] Trash icon does not appear on published rows.\n- [ ] Resume → Back from Step 2 → Step 1 form pre-filled, slug + version are read-only with the lock hint.\n- [ ] Edit a description in Step 1 → \"Save & continue\" → returns to Step 2 with bundle still uploaded (no re-upload required).\n- [ ] Visiting `/publish/<other-user-extension>/edit` returns 404 (existence not leaked).\n- [ ] `bun run typecheck` / `bun run lint` / `bun run test` (253 tests) / `bun run build` all pass.\nEOF\n)\"",

24:## Web app layout
48:### RSC vs client islands

---
40:│   ├── publish/
42:│   │   └── new/page.tsx       # upload wizard
50:Server components render the static shell, fetch data via Drizzle, and stream HTML. Client components are reserved for interactive bits — filter chips, the install button, the upload wizard, the user menu — and are marked with `"use client"`. The pattern is "server frame, client islands."
95:- **Versions** — flat changelog from `listExtensionVersions` (status `ready` only, ordered by `publishedAt DESC`), with the latest entry tagged "Current".
98:The sidebar has two cards. `<ExtAboutCard>` renders publisher / version / size / license / updated / scope plus the homepage and repo links — every URL passes through `safeExternalUrl()` (allowlists `http(s):`, drops anything else) before reaching `href`. `<ExtRelatedList>` shows the four related extensions; it returns `null` when empty so an empty card never shows.
107:   ├──── createDraftExtension ────► server action  ──► extensions row (status=draft)
124:   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
138:- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
155:Both surfaces are thin wrappers around `recordInstall` in `lib/installs/record.ts`, which owns: extension lookup (by `id` from the web button or `slug` from the CLI), version resolution (an omitted version means "latest published" — `extension_versions` ordered by `publishedAt DESC` among `status='ready'`), the `installs` row insert, the `installed` collection upsert, and the atomic `downloadsCount` bump — all three writes go through `db.transaction`. Each call records a row, so `downloadsCount` is total install events; the `installed` collection is a separate, idempotent membership concept used by the UI's "Installed" view. Failures throw a typed `InstallError` (`extension_not_found`, `no_published_version`); wrappers translate to their surface (action union or HTTP status).
188:| `/api/upload/sign` | Web-internal — issues an R2 presigned PUT URL for the upload wizard |
196:publish wizard ──→ extensions (draft) ──→ files + extensionVersions ──→ Inngest scan
198:                          extensions (published, search_vector) ←──── reindex-search
207:Every write that affects what users see in the listing (publish, install) ends with a `revalidateTag` so the next browse render hits fresh data.
```

102	### Publish
103	
104	```text
105	Browser                          Vercel                          R2          Inngest
106	   │ form submit                    │                              │             │
107	   ├──── createDraftExtension ────► server action  ──► extensions row (status=draft)
108	   │                                │                              │             │
109	   ├──── upload bundle ─────────────│─── presigned PUT URL ───────►│             │
110	   │                                │                              │             │
111	   ├──── attachFile(versionId) ────► server action  ──► files row + extensionVersion
112	   │                                │                              │             │
113	   ├──── submitForReview ──────────► server action  ──► sendEvent("extension/scan.requested")
114	   │                                │                              │             │
115	   │                                │                              │  scan-bundle:
116	   │                                │                              │   download from R2,
117	   │                                │                              │   sha-256 checksum,
118	   │                                │                              │   parse manifest.toml,
119	   │                                │                              │   validate schema,
120	   │                                │                              │   mark version ready/rejected,
121	   │                                │                              │   sendEvent("extension/index.requested")
122	   │                                │                              │             │
123	   │                                │                              │  reindex-search:
124	   │                                │                              │   set extension visibility=published,
125	   │                                │                              │   revalidateTag("extensions")
126	```
127	
128	The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.
129	
130	A note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.
131	
132	### Version state transitions
133	
134	`lib/extensions/state.ts` owns every transition of `extensionVersions.status`, `files.scanStatus`, and `extensions.visibility`. Three functions:
135	
136	- `submit(versionId)` — moves a version from `pending` to `scanning` (called by `submitForReview` before it dispatches the scan event).
137	- `recordScanResult(versionId, fileId, result)` — applies the outcome of a bundle scan in one transaction; success sets `files.scanStatus='clean' + version.status='ready' + checksumSha256`, failure sets `'flagged' + 'rejected'`. The job calls this once with a discriminated `result`.
138	- `publishVersion(versionId)` — flips `extensions.visibility='published'` and stamps both rows with `publishedAt`, returning the `extensionId` for the job's downstream `revalidateTag` and `extension/published` event.
139	
140	`extensions.search_vector` is a Postgres `GENERATED ALWAYS ... STORED` column (see `drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql`); no application code writes it. The `reindex-search` job's earlier hand-rolled `to_tsvector` UPDATE was dead — the generated column always reflects the source content.
141	
142	### Install (CLI path)
143	
144	```text
145	agentcenter install my-skill
146	  ├─ GET /api/v1/extensions/my-skill                     metadata (name, version, category)
```

352	
353	## 7. Upload pipeline
354	
355	```
356	[Publisher] /publish/new  (3-step wizard, client component)
357	  Step 1: Manifest form  (name, slug, category, scope, funcCat/subCat/l2, dept,
358	                          tags, description × 2 locales, install paths per agent)
359	            → Zod validate → server action createDraftExtension() → returns extensionId
360	  Step 2: File upload     POST /api/upload/sign  → R2 presigned PUT
361	                          → browser uploads bundle directly to R2
362	                          → server action attachFile(extensionId, fileId)
363	  Step 3: Review & submit → server action submitForReview(extensionId)
364	                          → inngest.send({ name: 'extension/scan.requested', ... })
365	                          → Extension stays visibility='draft', version status='pending'
366	                          → return to /publish dashboard
367	
368	[Background, Inngest]
369	  on 'extension/scan.requested':
370	    1. fetch from R2
371	    2. checksum + size guard
372	    3. parse manifest.json against ManifestSchema (Zod)
373	    4. (optional) clamav scan
374	    5. write scanReport to files; on clean → status='ready'
375	    6. inngest.send({ name: 'extension/index.requested', ... })
376	  on 'extension/index.requested':
377	    1. UPDATE extensions SET searchVector = ...
378	    2. flip visibility='published' if all checks pass
379	    3. revalidateTag('extensions')
380	    4. inngest.send({ name: 'extension/published', ... })  // for downstream notifications later
381	```
382	
383	**Synchronous** (server action, returns < 200ms): manifest creation, presigned URL signing, attach-file, submitForReview enqueue.
384	**Async** (Inngest): bundle download, scan, validation, indexing, publish flip.
385	
386	The manifest spec lives in `docs/manifest-spec.md` (drafted in Phase 8 before the wizard). Includes:
387	- Identity: `slug`, `name`, `nameZh`, `version` (semver), `category`, `license`
388	- Discoverability: `tags`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2`, `scope`
389	- Content: `description`, `descriptionZh`, `readme.md`, `screenshots/`
```

  "old_string": "The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.\n\nA note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.\n\n### Version state transitions",
  "new_string": "The upload goes browser → R2 directly (presigned PUT) — Vercel only signs the URL. The Inngest webhook lives at `/api/inngest`. Job source: `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and `lib/jobs/reindex-search.ts`.\n\nA note on `scan-bundle`: download + checksum + manifest parsing live in a single `step.run` rather than separate steps, because Inngest serializes step return values across boundaries and `Buffer` doesn't survive that round-trip.\n\nR2 bundle keys are `bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip` (`bundleKey()` in `lib/storage/r2.ts`). Two consequences worth knowing: (1) the wizard locks `slug` and `version` in resume/edit mode whenever a bundle has been uploaded — letting either change would orphan the bundle at the old key — and (2) discarding a draft only deletes DB rows; the R2 object is left for bucket lifecycle to garbage-collect.\n\n### Resume, edit, and discard\n\nThe dashboard at `/[locale]/publish` shows each draft's stage (`Needs bundle upload` / `Ready to submit` / `Awaiting scan` / etc., derived from `extensionVersions.status` + `bundleFileId` via `rowAction()` in `lib/publish/row-action.ts`). Rows whose latest version is `pending` are clickable and link into `/publish/[id]/edit`, which loads the draft via `getDraft()` and mounts the same `<UploadWizard>` in resume mode — Step 1 form pre-filled, slug/version locked, initial step derived from whether a bundle is uploaded. Step 1's submit calls `updateDraftExtension()` instead of `createDraftExtension()`; the action refuses (`version_not_editable`) once status leaves `pending`.\n\nEach draft row also gets a `<DiscardButton>` that calls `discardDraft()` — owner-checked, draft-visibility-only, hard delete (FK cascades clean up versions/tags/files-row links). `getDraft` and `discardDraft` deliberately collapse non-owner into `not_found` so the public action contract can't be used to probe existence.\n\n### Version state transitions"
}
```

Upload pipeline\n\n```\n[Publisher] /publish/new  (3-step wizard, client component)\n  Step 1: Manifest form  (name, slug, category, scope, funcCat/subCat/l2, dept,\n                          tags, description × 2 locales, install paths per agent)\n            → Zod validate → server action createDraftExtension() → returns extensionId\n  Step 2: File upload     POST /api/upload/sign  → R2 presigned PUT\n                          → browser uploads bundle directly to R2\n                          → server action attachFile(extensionId, fileId)\n  Step 3: Review & submit → server action submitForReview(extensionId)\n                          → submit(versionId) flips status to 'scanning'\n                            (idempotent: pending|scanning → scanning, so a retry\n                             after a failed inngest.send can re-queue the scan)\n                          → inngest.send({ name: 'extension/scan.requested', ... })\n                            (best-effort rollback to 'pending' on send failure)\n                          → return to /publish dashboard\n\n[Publisher] /publish (dashboard)\n  - Lists my drafts with stage (needs upload / ready to submit / scanning / …).\n  - Resumable rows (status='pending') link to /publish/[id]/edit → same wizard,\n    pre-filled, slug+version locked while a bundle is uploaded (they form the\n    R2 bundle key bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip).\n  - Per-row Discard button hard-deletes the draft (FK cascades). R2 bundle\n    objects are left for bucket lifecycle.\n\n[Background, Inngest]"
}

> AGENT

Just one actionable, but it's a real bug. The reviewer caught it: `updateDraftExtension` writes `data.slug` and `data.version` straight from the client payload. The `ManifestForm` `readOnly` lock is a client-side hint only — anyone with devtools (or hitting the action directly) can pass new values, the DB updates them, and the R2 bundle becomes orphaned at the *old* `bundles/<slug>/<version>/bundle.zip` key.

Server must enforce the lock. Simplest fix: drop `slug` and `version` from the UPDATE — never trust the client payload for these. Adding a regression test too.

{

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish-ownership.test.ts",
  "old_string": "  it(\"runs the update transaction when caller owns a pending draft\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    transactionMock.mockResolvedValue(undefined);\n\n    const result = await updateDraftExtension(\"ext-1\", validValues);\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: true });\n    expect(transactionMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n});",
  "new_string": "  it(\"runs the update transaction when caller owns a pending draft\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n    transactionMock.mockResolvedValue(undefined);\n\n    const result = await updateDraftExtension(\"ext-1\", validValues);\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: true });\n    expect(transactionMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n\n  // Regression: the wizard renders slug + version as `readOnly` in\n  // resume mode, but readOnly is client-side only. The action must NOT\n  // write slug or version from the request payload — otherwise a\n  // direct call (devtools, scripted POST) could rename the slug and\n  // orphan the already-uploaded R2 bundle at its old <slug>/<version>\n  // key.\n  it(\"does not write slug or version even when the payload tries to change them\", async () => {\n    getSessionMock.mockResolvedValue({ user: { id: \"user-A\" } });\n    selectMock.mockReturnValue(\n      selectChain([\n        {\n          publisherUserId: \"user-A\",\n          versionId: \"v-1\",\n          versionStatus: \"pending\",\n        },\n      ]),\n    );\n\n    // Capture the patches the transaction body issues against `tx.update`.\n    const updatePatches: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];\n    transactionMock.mockImplementation(\n      async (cb: (tx: unknown) => Promise<unknown>) => {\n        const tx = {\n          update: () => ({\n            set: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {\n              updatePatches.push(patch);\n              return { where: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) };\n            },\n          }),\n          delete: () => ({ where: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) }),\n          insert: () => ({ values: () => Promise.resolve(undefined) }),\n        };\n        return cb(tx);\n      },\n    );\n\n    const attempted = {\n      ...validValues,\n      slug: \"hijacked-slug\",\n      version: \"9.9.9\",\n    };\n    const result = await updateDraftExtension(\"ext-1\", attempted);\n    expect(result).toEqual({ ok: true });\n\n    // The wizard's update should produce exactly one extensions patch\n    // and zero version-row patches — slug + version aren't written.\n    expect(updatePatches).toHaveLength(1);\n    const patch = updatePatches[0];\n    expect(patch).not.toHaveProperty(\"slug\");\n    expect(patch).not.toHaveProperty(\"version\");\n    // Sanity: the editable fields are still going through.\n    expect(patch.name).toBe(\"My Skill\");\n  });\n});"
}

# Accessibility memo — items needing design input

Companion to the static a11y sweep (PR #16). The fixes that didn't need design
input are already applied: i18n on hardcoded ARIA labels and filter copy,
`aria-expanded` / `aria-pressed` / `role="group"` across toggleable widgets,
`role="search"` and an `aria-label` on the topbar search input, a skip-to-content
link, and converting the sidebar's "Installed / Saved" rows from focusable
buttons-that-do-nothing into non-interactive placeholders.

What follows is the punch-list that needs a product or design call before code
moves.

## 1. UserButton popover → Base UI Menu primitive

`components/layout/user-button.tsx` is a hand-rolled popover (click-outside via
`mousedown`, no `Esc` key handler, no focus trap, no focus restore on close, no
`role="menu"` / `role="menuitem"` semantics).

A11y fix: migrate to `@base-ui/react/menu` (already used elsewhere — see
`components/ui/select.tsx` for the wrapper pattern). Base UI gives us:
```

> AGENT

Phase 13 polish is mostly already done — `loading.tsx` / `error.tsx` exist for `[locale]`, `extensions`, `extensions/[slug]`; OG images for home + detail; a11y pass landed in PR #16 with a punch-list memo for design-decisions in `docs/a11y-memo.md`.

What's actually still open, ranked:

1. **Publish-surface polish** — `loading.tsx` + `error.tsx` for `/publish`, `/publish/new`, `/publish/[id]/edit` are missing. The pattern's set elsewhere; ~30 lines per route. Smallest gap-filler.
2. **Rejected / scanning UX** — once a version goes to `rejected`, the user has no UI to see *why* (no review-feedback surface) and no way to bump a version. `scanning` versions also have no progress indicator. More substantial.
3. **A11y memo punch-list** — `docs/a11y-memo.md` has documented items waiting for design calls (UserButton menu, etc.). Concrete but design-input gated.
4. **Memory bookkeeping** — I'd save (a) Inngest dev-server requirement (b) R2 CORS via Cloudflare dashboard (admin-token gate) (c) migration 0002 needs `bun run db:apply-fts`. None of that's discoverable from code alone.

My take: **(4) memory first** (3 minutes, zero-risk, helps future me), then **(1) publish polish** as the natural finish to the publish arc. (2) is meatier and probably its own milestone — not a casual next step.

Which way?

> DEVELOPER

can we do them all?

> AGENT

Let me be specific about scope before signing on to "all four":

- **(4) Memory** — actually doesn't need a PR; I save to my own memory dir. I'll do it now alongside the work. Zero scope.
- **(1) Publish polish** — small, ~3 routes × 2 files = ~150 lines total. PR-sized.
- **(2) Rejected/scanning UX** — meaty. The full version (rejection reason rendering, retry flow, version bumping) is a feature, not polish — probably its own milestone with design decisions. But a *focused slice* (scan-status badge with last-update time, rejection reason shown read-only on the resume page) is ~100 lines and complements the dashboard. I'd cap (2) at that focused slice.
- **(3) A11y memo** — every item in `docs/a11y-memo.md` is explicitly tagged "needs product or design call before code moves." I shouldn't plow through unilaterally. Skip until you want to walk through those decisions.

So my proposal: **save memory now, then one PR covering (1) publish polish + the focused slice of (2)**. Skip (3) until design-input session. Sound right?

> DEVELOPER

ok, one PR and several commits, is it ok?

> AGENT

Yes, one PR with two commits. Saving memory first (separate from the repo), then working on the PR.

  "content": "---\nname: Inngest dev server required for local Step 3 of publish wizard\ndescription: When working on the publish flow locally, Inngest dev server must be running or Step 3 fails with `scan_queue_unavailable`\ntype: project\n---\n\nThe Inngest client in `lib/jobs/client.ts` is configured with\n`isDev: process.env.NODE_ENV !== \"production\"`, which routes events to\n`http://localhost:8288`. Step 3 of the publish wizard\n(`submitForReview`) calls `inngest.send(...)`; if no Inngest dev server\nis running on that port, the call throws and the action returns\n`scan_queue_unavailable` with a best-effort `scanning -> pending`\nrollback.\n\n**Why:** Inngest cloud requires `INNGEST_EVENT_KEY`; we don't gate\nlocal dev on that.\n**How to apply:** When the user reports Step 3 failing locally, ask\nwhether they have `npx inngest-cli@latest dev` running in another\nterminal before chasing other causes. Without it the wizard surfaces\nthe correct `scan_queue_unavailable` message but the underlying issue\nis just \"dev server not running.\"\n"
}

Cloudflare R2 requires a bucket-level admin token for that\ncall. The standard \"Object Read & Write\" R2 API token used in\n`.env.local` returns `403 AccessDenied`.\n\n**Why:** The user (and presumably any future dev) creates an\nObject R/W token because that's what the upload-sign route needs.\nReusing it for CORS application doesn't work.\n**How to apply:** When CORS needs setting up, point the user at the\nCloudflare dashboard (Bucket → Settings → CORS Policy) with the JSON\nfrom the README / earlier conversation rather than running the script.\nThe script remains useful for anyone with an admin-scoped token\n(future P1 ops automation), but isn't the primary path for dev setup.\nThe CORS rules to paste:\n\n```json\n[\n  { \"AllowedOrigins\": [\"http://localhost:3000\"], \"AllowedMethods\": [\"PUT\"],\n    \"AllowedHeaders\": [\"Content-Type\",\"Content-Length\",\"Content-MD5\"],\n    \"ExposeHeaders\": [\"ETag\"], \"MaxAgeSeconds\": 3600 },\n  { \"AllowedOrigins\": [\"*\"], \"AllowedMethods\": [\"GET\",\"HEAD\"],\n    \"MaxAgeSeconds\": 86400 }\n]\n```\n"
}

  "content": "---\nname: drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql is hand-applied, not journaled\ndescription: Migration 0002 isn't tracked in drizzle/meta/_journal.json; fresh DBs need `bun run db:apply-fts`\ntype: project\n---\n\n`drizzle/0002_fts_search_vector.sql` adds a Postgres `GENERATED ALWAYS\n... STORED` tsvector column plus pg_trgm GIN indexes. drizzle-kit can't\ninfer those from the schema, so the file is hand-authored and\ndeliberately not listed in `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`. `drizzle-kit\nmigrate` skips it.\n\n**Why:** When a user runs `bun run db:migrate` and then hits a\n`column \"search_vector\" of relation \"extensions\" does not exist` (PG\n42703) on the publish flow, the migration silently didn't apply.\n**How to apply:** The fix is `bun run db:apply-fts` (script:\n`scripts/apply-fts.ts`, executes the SQL via the existing `db` client;\nSQL is `IF NOT EXISTS`-guarded so it's safe to re-run). Mention this\nwhen the user is setting up a fresh DB or hitting that error code.\n"
}

  "content": "---\nname: Direct, high-iteration collaboration style\ndescription: User confirms with one-liners (\"ok\", \"go ahead\", \"commit\"); wants Plan A first then B, split commits per concern, tests + docs alongside code, CodeRabbit reply + merge per PR\n---\n\nAcross PRs #19-#22 on this repo the user established a clear pattern:\n\n- **Confirms briefly, doesn't redirect lightly.** \"ok\", \"go ahead\",\n  \"commit\", \"what's next?\" — they want forward motion. Don't bury them\n  in long plans; give 2-3 sentences with a recommendation and proceed\n  on confirmation.\n- **Trusts the smaller-first heuristic, even when initially drawn to\n  the bigger one.** Floated Plan B (full edit on resume), accepted\n  Plan A (resume only), then asked for B as a follow-up. Worth\n  pushing back on Plan B-style instincts when there are clear\n  correctness traps and offering \"we can add later if real users want\n  it.\"\n- **Wants split commits per concern in one PR**, not one mega-commit.\n  Two-to-three commit PRs are the norm.\n- **Wants tests added when the change is meaningful** (security\n  contracts, state-machine routing) but not for trivia.\n- **Wants docs (CLAUDE.md says architecture.md / plan.md) updated in\n  the same PR when binding decisions change.**\n- **Standard PR cycle**: open → CodeRabbit replies addressed in a\n  follow-up commit → reply on the PR thread → merge. Each PR ends\n  with a \"want me to merge?\" confirmation, not auto-merge.\n\n**Why:** This makes review fast and merges low-anxiety; matches the\nproject's CLAUDE.md (\"Commit per coherent unit\"; \"Always work on a\nfeature branch and open a PR\").\n**How to apply:** Default to this rhythm without re-litigating it.\nDon't ask \"do you want tests?\" when the change has a security or\nstate-machine surface — propose the tests and proceed on a \"go\nahead.\" Don't bundle unrelated polish into a focused PR even if\nthey say \"do all of these\"; counter-propose the split.\n"
}

"use client";

import { AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { useEffect } from "react";

interface Props {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}

export default function ExtensionsListingError({ error, reset }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations("extensions.errorBoundary");

  useEffect(() => {
    console.error("[extensions/listing]", error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="px-7 py-12">
      <div className="border-border bg-card/40 mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col items-center gap-4 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-10 text-center">
        <AlertCircle className="text-destructive size-8" />
        <h2 className="text-foreground text-base font-semibold">
          {t("title")}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground max-w-sm text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed">
          {t("body")}
        </p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={reset}
          className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 mt-1 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-opacity"
        >
          {t("retry")}
        </button>
        {error.digest && (
          <code className="text-muted-foreground mt-1 font-mono text-[10.5px]">
            {error.digest}
          </code>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/app/[locale]/publish/error.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { AlertCircle } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\nimport { useEffect } from \"react\";\n\nimport { Link } from \"@/lib/i18n/navigation\";\n\ninterface Props {\n  error: Error & { digest?: string };\n  reset: () => void;\n}\n\n// Single error boundary for /publish, /publish/new, and\n// /publish/[id]/edit. Next.js cascades a parent `error.tsx` into nested\n// segments unless they own one — these three surfaces share the same\n// recovery affordance (retry + back to dashboard) so one boundary fits.\nexport default function PublishError({ error, reset }: Props) {\n  const t = useTranslations(\"publish.errorBoundary\");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    console.error(\"[publish]\", error);\n  }, [error]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"px-7 py-12\">\n      <div className=\"border-border bg-card/40 mx-auto flex max-w-md flex-col items-center gap-4 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-10 text-center\">\n        <AlertCircle className=\"text-destructive size-8\" />\n        <h2 className=\"text-foreground text-base font-semibold\">\n          {t(\"title\")}\n        </h2>\n        <p className=\"text-muted-foreground max-w-sm text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed\">\n          {t(\"body\")}\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"mt-1 flex gap-2\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={reset}\n            className=\"bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:opacity-90 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-opacity\"\n          >\n            {t(\"retry\")}\n          </button>\n          <Link\n            href=\"/publish\"\n            className=\"border-border text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted rounded-md border px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-semibold transition-colors\"\n          >\n            {t(\"back\")}\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n        {error.digest && (\n          <code className=\"text-muted-foreground mt-1 font-mono text-[10.5px]\">\n            {error.digest}\n          </code>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/actions/publish.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {\n  // Pull the latest version per extension alongside the extension row so the\n  // dashboard can show \"where am I in the wizard\" (e.g. needs upload vs.\n  // submitted, awaiting scan) — visibility alone hides that info.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));\n\n  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the\n  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per\n  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.\n  const byExtension = new Map<string, (typeof rows)[number]>();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) byExtension.set(row.id, row);\n  }\n  return Array.from(byExtension.values());\n}",
  "new_string": "// Shape produced by `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` and stored in\n// `files.scanReport`. Extracted into a small extractor below so the\n// dashboard doesn't have to know the JSON layout.\nfunction extractScanReason(report: unknown): string | null {\n  if (\n    typeof report === \"object\" &&\n    report !== null &&\n    \"reason\" in report &&\n    typeof (report as { reason: unknown }).reason === \"string\"\n  ) {\n    return (report as { reason: string }).reason;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport async function getMyExtensions(userId: string) {\n  // Pull the latest version per extension alongside the extension row so the\n  // dashboard can show \"where am I in the wizard\" (e.g. needs upload vs.\n  // submitted, awaiting scan) — visibility alone hides that info. Also\n  // joins the bundle file via `bundleFileId` (logical reference, no FK)\n  // so the dashboard can surface the scan rejection reason inline for\n  // rejected versions without an extra round-trip.\n  const rows = await db\n    .select({\n      id: extensions.id,\n      slug: extensions.slug,\n      name: extensions.name,\n      category: extensions.category,\n      visibility: extensions.visibility,\n      createdAt: extensions.createdAt,\n      latestVersionId: extensionVersions.id,\n      latestVersion: extensionVersions.version,\n      latestStatus: extensionVersions.status,\n      latestBundleFileId: extensionVersions.bundleFileId,\n      latestScanReport: files.scanReport,\n    })\n    .from(extensions)\n    .leftJoin(\n      extensionVersions,\n      eq(extensionVersions.extensionId, extensions.id),\n    )\n    .leftJoin(files, eq(files.id, extensionVersions.bundleFileId))\n    .where(eq(extensions.publisherUserId, userId))\n    .orderBy(desc(extensions.createdAt), desc(extensionVersions.createdAt));\n\n  // Collapse to one row per extension (latest version first thanks to the\n  // ORDER BY). If we someday support multiple in-flight versions per\n  // extension, this becomes a `DISTINCT ON` query.\n  const byExtension = new Map<\n    string,\n    (typeof rows)[number] & { latestScanReason: string | null }\n  >();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    if (!byExtension.has(row.id)) {\n      byExtension.set(row.id, {\n        ...row,\n        latestScanReason: extractScanReason(row.latestScanReport),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n  return Array.from(byExtension.values());\n}"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/publish/scan-report.ts",
  "content": "// Defensive extractor for the bundle-scan report stored on `files.scanReport`.\n//\n// `lib/jobs/scan-bundle.ts` writes a `{ manifestOk, reason, checksum }`\n// JSON blob today, but the column type is `jsonb` and there's no Zod\n// schema gating writes — anything could land here in the future\n// (older rows from before the format settled, the worker getting\n// extended, manual SQL fixes). The dashboard renders the rejection\n// reason inline, so we extract narrowly and return `null` for anything\n// that doesn't match the expected shape rather than crashing.\n\nexport function extractScanReason(report: unknown): string | null {\n  if (\n    typeof report === \"object\" &&\n    report !== null &&\n    \"reason\" in report &&\n    typeof (report as { reason: unknown }).reason === \"string\" &&\n    (report as { reason: string }).reason.length > 0\n  ) {\n    return (report as { reason: string }).reason;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/publish/scan-report.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\nimport { extractScanReason } from \"./scan-report\";\n\ndescribe(\"extractScanReason\", () => {\n  it(\"returns the reason string when present and non-empty\", () => {\n    expect(\n      extractScanReason({\n        manifestOk: false,\n        reason: \"manifest.toml missing required `name` field\",\n        checksum: \"abc123\",\n      }),\n    ).toBe(\"manifest.toml missing required `name` field\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns null when reason is null (clean scan)\", () => {\n    expect(\n      extractScanReason({\n        manifestOk: true,\n        reason: null,\n        checksum: \"abc123\",\n      }),\n    ).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns null when reason is missing entirely\", () => {\n    expect(extractScanReason({ manifestOk: true, checksum: \"abc\" })).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns null when reason is the wrong type\", () => {\n    expect(extractScanReason({ reason: 42 })).toBeNull();\n    expect(extractScanReason({ reason: { nested: \"x\" } })).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns null for an empty string reason (don't render an empty label)\", () => {\n    expect(extractScanReason({ reason: \"\" })).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns null for non-object inputs (legacy nulls, scalar values)\", () => {\n    expect(extractScanReason(null)).toBeNull();\n    expect(extractScanReason(undefined)).toBeNull();\n    expect(extractScanReason(\"just a string\")).toBeNull();\n    expect(extractScanReason(42)).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n"
}

Rows whose latest version is `pending` are clickable and link into `/publish/[id]/edit`, which loads the draft via `getDraft()` and mounts the same `<UploadWizard>` in resume mode — Step 1 form pre-filled, slug/version locked, initial step derived from whether a bundle is uploaded. {
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter/lib/publish/scan-report.ts",
  "old_string": "export function extractScanReason(report: unknown): string | null {\n  if (\n    typeof report === \"object\" &&\n    report !== null &&\n    \"reason\" in report &&\n    typeof (report as { reason: unknown }).reason === \"string\" &&\n    (report as { reason: string }).reason.length > 0\n  ) {\n    return (report as { reason: string }).reason;\n  }\n  return null;\n}",
  "new_string": "export function extractScanReason(report: unknown): string | null {\n  if (\n    typeof report === \"object\" &&\n    report !== null &&\n    \"reason\" in report &&\n    typeof (report as { reason: unknown }).reason === \"string\"\n  ) {\n    // Trim before length-checking so a whitespace-only reason\n    // (e.g. `\"   \"`) doesn't render as an empty label-with-no-text.\n    const reason = (report as { reason: string }).reason.trim();\n    if (reason.length > 0) return reason;\n  }\n  return null;\n}"
}
```
